金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 8 月 31 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國通膨仍高，民眾實質消費呈現停滯：美國 7 月個人消費支出物價指數較上月上升 0.2%、
較去年同期上升 3.7%；扣除波動較大的食品與能源後，核心指數也月增 0.2%、年增 3.3%，
顯示物價壓力仍未明顯消退。同月個人所得月增 0.4%、可支配所得月增 0.5%，但名目消費
支出只成長 0.2%，扣除物價後的實質消費增幅不到 0.1%，顯示美國家庭收入雖有成長，但
卻沒有明顯轉化為實質消費。
◼ NVIDIA 第二季營收翻倍：NVIDIA 8 月 26 日公布 2027 會計年度第二季財報，營收 962 億

美元，較前一季成長 18%，並較去年同期成長 106%；其中資料中心業務營收 890 億美元，
年增 117%，反映大型雲端業者與 AI 公司仍持續擴建運算中心。 NVIDIA 財報表現亮眼，顯
示全球 AI 基礎建設投資仍未降溫，有利整體 AI 類股市場情緒。
◼ 美國擴大對伊朗經濟制裁：美國財政部 8 月 24 日啟動「經濟放逐行動」 ，意圖切斷伊朗政府
與伊斯蘭革命衛隊海外資金來源。首波措施制裁近 60 個協助伊朗取得核子與飛彈技術、發
動網路攻擊或銷售石油的實體、個人與船舶，並將數位資產、科技、黃金等納入次級制裁範
圍。儘管美國擴大對伊朗經濟制裁，伊朗仍保持強硬態度，短期內美伊衝突不確定性仍高。
市場展望與操作策略
◼ 通膨改善有限，股市震盪料將加劇：美國 7 月 PCE 年增率略高於市場預期，顯示通膨降溫進
展有限，加上第二季美國內需仍具韌性，帶動市場對 Fed 年底前的升息預期上升，利率政策
方向不確定性料將使股市波動加大，同時統計顯示，9 月股市歷史表現多偏弱，且適逢美國
期中選舉前，市場資金料將保持觀望態度，故在投資策略上不宜太過激進，布局仍以半導

體、AI 基礎建設類股及台、日市場為主。
◼ 3～10 年期公債與投資級債條件相對有利：美國財政部擴大長天期公債回購，一度帶動長端
殖利率回落，但通膨與需求數據仍具韌性，使年底前市場升息預期再度升溫。市場對美國財
政赤字、公債供給及長期通膨仍要求較高風險補償，30 年期殖利率與短天期利差仍維持高
檔，顯示期限溢酬尚未收斂，考量當前 3～10 年期美國公債與投資級債能鎖定目前較高息
收，且其受長端財政風險影響也相對較小，故現階段 3～10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風險
報酬結構相對較佳。

2026 年 8 月 18 日

調査レポート

2026 年 4～6 月期の GDP（1 次速報）結果

～イラン情勢の悪化が逆風も、3 四半期連続のプラス成長～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田隼平

2026 年 8 月 17 日に内閣府が公表した 2026 年 4～6 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比＋0.3%（年
率換算＋1.1%）と、3 四半期連続のプラス成長となった。特に外需寄与度が前期比＋0.5%ポイントと 3 四半期連
続でプラスとなり、成長率を押し上げた。輸出が 3 四半期連続で増加する中、輸入はイラン情勢の悪化を受けて
鉱物性燃料を中心に 2 四半期ぶりの減少となった。 
一方、内需寄与度は▲0.2%ポイントと 3 四半期ぶりのマイナスとなった。政府最終消費は高校授業料無償化
の拡充や小学校給食費の無償化などを背景に増加したものの、個人消費や住宅投資、設備投資は減少し、政
府による石油備蓄の取り崩しを受けて公的在庫も減少した。 
もっとも、内需が力強さを欠いたとはいえ、政府の物価高対策や代替調達の進展などが下支えとなる中で、イ
ラン情勢の悪化によって当初懸念された物価高や供給制約が景気全体を大きく下押しする事態は回避された。
先行きについても緩やかな回復基調が続くと期待されるが、米国とイランの対立長期化に伴う資源価格の高止ま
りや供給制約の再燃には引き続き注意が必要だろう。 
なお、物価上昇分を含んだ名目 GDP は、前期比＋1.2%（年率換算＋4.8%）と、9 四半期連続のプラス成長と
なった。物価上昇圧力は依然として強く、コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進む中で、 GDP デフレーターは前年比  
＋2.6%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.9%）と、高い伸びが続いた。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度（%ポイント）

### Page 2

2 / 2

需要項目別の動向は以下の通りである。

① 実質個人消費（前期比－ 0.0%） 
 家計を取り巻く所得環境は改善したものの（実質雇用者報酬は前期比＋ 0.8% ）、イラン情勢の
悪化による消費者マインドの弱さもあり、 8 四半期ぶりに減少した。特に非耐久財（前期比   
－1.8%）が加熱式たばこの値上げもあって落ち込んだほか、サービス（同－ 0.1%）も小幅に減
少した。他方で、制度変更や価格上昇前の駆け込みが一部に生じた影響もあって、耐久財（前
期比＋2.8%）や半耐久財（同＋ 1.1%）は増加した。  
② 実質住宅投資（前期比－ 0.5%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数は 2 四半期ぶりに増加したものの、床面積ベースでは伸び
悩んでいることもあり、住宅投資は 3 四半期ぶりに減少した。  
③ 実質設備投資（前期比－ 1.2%） 
 企業の投資意欲は良好なものの、建設費をはじめ種々の投資コストが増加していることもあって、
設備投資は 2 四半期連続で減少した。  
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度＋ 0.3%ポイント）  
 2 月末からのイラン情勢悪化による供給制約懸念の高まりを受けて、メーカーが製品在庫の積
み増しを進めたことから、民間在庫はプラス寄与となった。  
 なお、公的在庫は政府が石油備蓄の放出を進めたことから、前期比寄与度－ 0.5%ポイントとな
った。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費（前期比＋ 1.6%） 
 高校授業料無償化の拡充や小学校給食費の無償化などもあり、前期比で増加が続いた。  
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比－ 0.1%） 
 名目では増加したものの、デフレーターの上昇には追いついておらず、 2 四半期ぶりに減少し
た。 
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度＋ 0.5%ポイント、実質輸出：前期比＋ 0.5%、実質輸入：同－ 1.5%） 
 輸出は前期比＋ 0.5%と増加した。財はイラン情勢の悪化を受けて中東向けを中心に減少した
一方、サービスは研究開発サービスが一時的な要因により上振れたこともあって増加した。  
 輸入は前期比－ 1.5%と減少した。財は鉱物性燃料を中心に減少し、サービスもこのところ増加
が続いていた反動もあって減少した。  
 結果、輸出から輸入を差し引いた外需の寄与度は＋ 0.5%ポイントとプラスになった。

## Document 059: 260831_daiwa_ Kinsus

Source PDF: all report pdf/260831_daiwa_ Kinsus.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: We hosted an online investor conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 
31 August. We believe that management’s current revenue target is 15% QoQ growth in 
3Q26 (vs. market expectation of 10% QoQ). Regarding the investigation into Unimicron’s 
original place of production, the company noted that it has not seen any order shift from 
end customers. However, it believes this event will not be a negative factor for other 
substrate makers. In addition, Kinsus’ 9% share price decline today has nothing to do with 
the investigation into Unimicron. Rather, it is more likely related to its parent company’s 
plan to sell 16 million shares in Kinsus (announced last Friday). Kinsus believes this was 
driven by financial management considerations, and not by any specific intention to reduce 
its stake in Kinsus. If Kinsus’ share price continues to rise, management does not rule out 
the possibility that its parent company may trim its stake again. Overall, we believe Kinsus’ 
fundamentals remain largely in line with our expectations, with raw material prices 
continuing to rise and substrate supply tightening gradually. We maintain our Buy (1) rating 
on the stock, with a 12-month target price of TWD1,068, based on a 36x PER on the 1-
year forward EPS. Key downside risks include weaker-than-expected demand for memory, 
PCs, gaming GPUs, and AI servers.

We hosted an online conference call for Kinsus after trading hours on 31 August. Overall, 
we believe the business outlook to be largely on track with our previous note (link here, 
published on 31 July). Below are our key takeaways from the conference.

Recent updates:  
• We believe the company has revised up its 3Q26 revenue growth target from 10% QoQ 
to 15% QoQ, reflecting a better-than-expected benefit from price increases. Looking 
ahead to 4Q26, management still expects growth of 10% QoQ, in line with its previous 
comments. 
• Revenue mix in 2Q26: ABF 45%, BT 38%, and contact lens 17%. Looking ahead, the 
company expects the revenue mix from ABF to gradually increase to 50–55% in 2027.

Price hike & margin expansion: 
• The company maintains its target of a 7-8% ASP increase per quarter in 2H26 to reflect 
rising costs of raw materials, including glass fibre, copper foil, drill bits, and ABF build-up 
film. 
• Although some competitors have highlighted a much larger magnitude of price hikes, 
Kinsus has not seen a similar level of increase. The company also believes the price 
hike level for ABF will be higher than that for BT in 2H26, whereas in 1H26 the price hike 
for BT was higher than that for ABF. 
• Looking into 2H26 and 2027, the company expects raw material ASPs to continue rising. 
As such, single-digit price hikes should be possible, with raw materials currently 
accounting for 30% of BT’s COGS. In addition, the driver of ASP increases is expected 
to gradually shift from raw material inflation to substrate capacity shortages.  
• In terms of gross margin expansion, Kinsus believes this will be driven mainly by an 
improved product mix and rising utilisation rates, rather than price hikes. This is similar 
to Unimicron. However, NYPCB’s gross margin expansion could be driven by internal 
price hikes within the Nanya Group.

### Page 2

Kinsus Interconnect Technology (3189 TT) 
31 August 2026 
2

Utilisation rates (UTR): 
• ABF: The company expects the UTR to increase from 85% (beginning of 2026) to 95% 
(at the end of 2026). 
• BT: The company expects the UTR to increase from 75% (beginning of 2026) to 85% (at 
the end of 2026).

Capacity expansion plan 
• Given capacity constraints, Kinsus will gradually expand its ABF capacity. It currently 
has around a 40 million ABF substrate capacity per month and plans to increase this to 
80 million by 2029. 
• According to management, the major equipment pull-in cycle will occur in 2027. After 
that, most equipment will be subsidised by end customers, and the depreciation 
expense should decline to TWD6.5-7.0bn from 2028 onward, versus TWD7.1-7.2bn in 
2026 and 2027. 
• As depreciation currently accounts for 15% of COGS, Kinsus believes it will reduce its 
selling prices slightly after clients begin subsidising the equipment, as the benefit will be 
shared between clients and Kinsus, at 7-8% each.

New growth opportunities – ABF 
• We believe Kinsus will secure a higher allocation for both CPU and GPU products from 
a global leading GPU brand in its next-generation products, given the current industry’s 
supply constraints. 
• The company also believes it will provide additional support to other GPU brands for 
their GPU products from 2027. 
• Outside of GPUs, the company sees less progress in the ASIC segment for now 
compared with GPUs. It has secured around a 10% allocation for one major ASIC 
product, but progress in other ASIC products remains relatively limited.

New growth opportunities – BT  
• The company believes pluggable optical modules and market share gains (as 
competitors gradually exit the BT market) will be key growth drivers for Kinsus. 
• According to management, the BT substrate used in optical modules will have 14 layers, 
compared with the mainstream 4-layer format today. Its existing equipment can support 
both 4-layer and 14-layer BT substrates. However, the company may need to add some 
routing capacity to support optical module demand.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

EV business in August 2026: Hon Hai’s EV subsidiary Foxtron (2258.TW, not covered) 
reported Aug revenues of NT$914mn (vs. Jul-26 / Aug-25 revenues at NT$598mn / 
NT$111mn). Post the announcement of its proposal to acquire Luxgen in December 
(report), Foxtron has announced its first self-branded vehicle “Foxtron Bria”. Also, the 
partnership with Mitsubishi Motors (7211.T, covered by Kota Yuzawa) on passenger cars 
(read more in our report) and zero emission buses (read more on the company’s 
announcement) suggests scope for incremental growth opportunities for Foxtron which 
could be positive for Hon Hai.

Earnings revision: We factor in Hon Hai Aug revenues and revise down our 2026E 
earnings by 1%, mainly on lower revenues; meanwhile we remain positive on gradual AI 
server ramp up and smartphones spec upgrade to drive growth ahead. We keep 2027E/ 
2028E estimates largely unchanged.

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and remains based on a 
P/E. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 18.9x (unchanged) is derived from a peers’ 
average ratio of trading P/E to forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the 
sum of NI YoY and OPM). Our target P/E of 18.9x is in the range of the company’s

historical forward P/E since 2010, reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on an 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Chenbro  | Asia Pacific
August Sales Flat MoM/+41%
Y oY
In this report, we focus on Chenbro's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August  sales were NT$2,457mn (flat MoM/+41% YoY).
• QTD sales reached 59% of our 3Q26 estimate at NT$8,368mn (+7% QoQ/
+48% YoY) and 56% of consensus at NT$8,763mn (+12% QoQ/+55% YoY).
Our view:
• We see its 3Q26 revenue tracking slightly behind our estimate.
• We think near-term sentiment should remain lackluster amid the temporary

hiccup resulting from supply chain management.
• Management remains optimistic on the 2H26 outlook. The demand from its
GPU/ASIC server clusters and general servers is strong, driving  orders for its
chassis and rack offerings.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Chenbro (8210.TW)
Base case, derived from 27x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by the 33%
earnings CAGR we see for 2025-28, which implies a PEG of 0.8. This is in line the average of
0.8 over 2014-24, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 12x for a 15% earnings
CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger AI capex outlook
n Larger market share gains within existing customers

n Faster penetration into new customers
n Higher entry barrier among peers that leads to higher ASP/profitability
n Better macro outlook
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Less share gains within existing customers
n Slower penetration into new customers
n Intensifying competition leading to lower ASP/profitability
n Continuous macro uncertainties

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy ,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover . Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy , hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety , and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk -adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below .
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below .
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

MPI Corporation  | Asia Pacific
3Q Revenue Looks Better than
Feared; OW
MPI reported August sales of NT$2,048mn, +13% M/M and +70% Y/Y, above our
NT$1,876mn estimate –  mainly supported by price hikes, TPU, and other AI ASICs.
Assuming flat September revenue M/M, 3Q revenue would rise 13% Q/Q, above our
+7% estimate and the Street at +10% Q/Q.
Stay OW: As previously indicated, we expect 3Q revenue to exceed guidance given
CPC/VPC price hikes. With very strong micro bump demand and more migration to

2nm, we believe MPI needs to expand MEMS probe card capacity to at least 8–10mn
pins per month in 2027, positioning it to meet strong AI ASIC and GPU demand.
PCB material shortages remain a risk; however, MPI is diversifying suppliers and
seeking substitutes.
For CPO,  Insertion 2 qualification continues, with insertion 3 shipments on track to
start in 4Q26.
Investors were slightly frustrated by the conservative guidance, but still view MPI as
best positioned to benefit from increased chip probing demand over the longer term

Key Takeaways
The stock could come under pressure following management's conservative
guidance. However, we expect more updates to follow in coming quarters.
Strong micro bump demand and greater migration to 2nm lead the team to argue
that MEMS probe card capacity should expand to 8–10mn pins per month in
2027.

suppliers/seeking substitutes to reduce reliance on constrained inputs.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 103: 260909_daiwa_ardentec-buy-initiation

Source PDF: all report pdf/260909_daiwa_ardentec-buy-initiation.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 24

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Ardentec, a Taiwanese pure 
testing provider serving foundries, IDMs and fabless chipmakers, with a 
Buy (1) rating. We believe Ardentec is transitioning from an auto/industrial 
test vendor into an AI/HPC wafer-sort powerhouse, supported by a 35% 
revenue CAGR (2025-28E) and gross margin expansion to 45% by 2028E.

Longtan fab ramp with premium hourly rate. We expect the 7-story 
Longtan mega-fab, which is developing at one floor per quarter to deliver 
significant revenue contribution (c.TWD450m+/quarter per floor). We 
believe this underpins a structural upgrade in revenue, as advanced wafer 
sorting for application-specific integrated circuits (ASIC) commands an 
hourly billing rate nearly double that of legacy testing. Furthermore, we 
believe strategic collaborations with key Taiwan foundry leaders position 
Ardentec to capture expanding AI/HPC test allocations moving forward.

Gross margin increase driven by high utilisation rate. As its key peers 
prioritise merchant AI GPUs, displaced mobile/RF/networking orders are 
refilling Ardentec’s fully depreciated tester lines, lifting mature utilisation. 
Test-time inflation (2-3x on complex ASICs) anchors Longtan capacity at a 
90%+ utilisation rate. As fixed costs comprise 40-45% of COGS, this drives 
blended gross margin from 36% in 2025 to 45% in 2028E, with steady 
operating expense (9-10% of sales) flowing into operating profit.

Turnkey E/O testing for CPO and non-dilutive expansion. Leveraging 
synergies with Giga-Solution, Raytek (7873 TT; 25% stake), and strategic 
partner Fabrinet (FN US, NR), Ardentec builds a comprehensive electro-
optical test flow aligned with TSMC’s compact universal photonic engine 
(COUPE) platform. While co-packaged optics (CPO) testing accounts for 
<5% of near-term sales, it secures an early-mover moat in optical I/O.

Catalysts: 1) Completion of 7-floor capacity with premium rates and higher 
UTR; 2) peer spillover orders refilling fully depreciated capacity, expanding 
gross margin; 3) higher-than-expected ASIC and CPO contribution.

Valuation: Driven by a structural earnings inflection fuelled by Longtan fab 
expansion, expanding AI ASIC test pricing premiums, and high-margin peer 
spillover orders, we initiate Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP 
of TWD340, based on a target PER of 25x applied to our 4-quarter-forward 
EPS, at the top end of its historical 10-25x range but supported by a 48% 
2025-28E EPS CAGR and peer AI test valuations of 18-24x.

Risks: 1) Delays in capacity expansion; 2) pricing pressure as a second 
supplier; and 3) slow automotive and industrial recovery.

### Page 3

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026

How do we justify our view? 
Growth outlook Valuation Earnings revisions

Growth outlook

Ardentec: revenue and gross margin outlook   
We forecast Ardentec to enter a structural multi-year 
revenue growth cycle, rising from TWD14.0bn in 2025 to 
TWD34.4bn by 2028E, implying a 34.8% CAGR. This 
revenue growth is supported by the phased ramp-up of its 
Longtan fab facility, increasing exposure to premium AI 
ASIC and HPC wafer testing, and broad-based ASP 
expansion driven by tighter mature-node test capacity. 
Meanwhile, we expect improving utilisation coupled with 
growing contributions from fully depreciated legacy testers, 
to drive gross margin expansion from 36.3% in 2025 to 
45.0% by 2028E.

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Valuation   Ardentec: 1-year-forward PER bands    
Given the current structural mix transition from cyclical 
automotives towards high-margin AI/HPC custom ASICs, 
amplified gross margin and operating leverage from the 
Longtan ramp, and ROE inflection likely to surpass its 2022 
historical peak of c.23% over 2026-28E, we initiate 
coverage of Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month 
TP of TWD340, based on a target PER of 25.0x applied to 
our 4-quarter-forward EPS, at the top end of its historical 
10-25x range but supported by a 48% 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR and peer AI test valuations of 18-24x.

Source: Bloomberg

Earnings revisions

Ardentec: Bloomberg-consensus earnings revisions    
Bloomberg consensus raised its 2026-27E EPS estimates 
by 30-45%, driven by the rapid Longtan Floor 1 ramp, 
premium ASIC hourly rates, and high-margin peer spillover 
orders. The upward revisions likely reflect the completion 
of the remaining floors, test-time inflation driving higher 
testing hours, and internal capacity tightness unlocking 
pricing power across mature lines. Ardentec is currently 
covered by 4 brokers. We believe the market is overlooking 
this stock despite its structural fundamental turnaround 
outlook and depressed valuation.

Source: Bloomberg

Company profile 
Kinsus was founded in 2000 as a subsidiary of Asustek. The company mainly focus on niche ball grid 
array (BGA) substrates to avoid price competition among mainstream products like graphics card and 
chipsets, and it has become top-3 flip chip manufacturer in global. Besides, its manufacturing sites are 
based in Taiwan and China.

### Page 6

6

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
From Automotive to AI/HPC growth engines 
A premier pure play in wafer testing 
Founded in October 1999 and headquartered in Hukou, Hsinchu, Ardentec Corporation is 
a premier semiconductor testing pure play, ranking among the top three pure test service 
providers in Taiwan in terms of revenue. While semiconductor testing is inherently a 
capital- and technology-intensive arena dominated by foundry and integrated OSAT titans 
such as TSMC (2330 TT, TWD2,465, Buy [1]; covered by Daiwa analyst Rick Hsu) and 
ASE Group (3711 TT, TWD640, Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Rick Hsu), 
Ardentec establishes a resilient, differentiated market presence through two structural 
pillars:

• Strategic dual-sourcing anchor and supply chain balancer. As advanced packaging 
and testing capacity is increasingly centralised within leading foundries and mega-
OSATs, tier-1 fabless leaders actively de-risk operations by qualifying secondary 
sources. Ardentec functions as an independent, pure-play testing alternative, providing 
foundry-aligned engineering standards alongside commercial flexibility. This positioning 
allows tier-1 chipmakers to diversify vendor concentration, secure dedicated capacity 
allocations during cyclical tight supply, and mitigate single-point manufacturing 
bottlenecks.

• Technical versatility supported by automotive qualification standards. Ardentec 
maintains a high revenue exposure to automotive and industrial IC testing – sectors that 
demand zero-defect reliability, ultra-wide temperature ranges, and complex mixed-signal 
test protocols. This operational baseline provides significant cross-application versatility. 
During automotive cycle downturns, qualified test cells and probe architectures can be 
reconfigured to support compute, edge AI, and high-frequency communication ICs, 
helping dampen gross margin volatility and stabilising baseline fleet utilisation across 
broader semiconductor cycles.

Spanning seven vertical cleanroom floors, the company’s Longtan facility began a multi-

in 3Q26; its +15% MoM revenue inflection in July validated the expansion. At peak

predictable one-floor-per-quarter cadence, each incremental floor can accommodate a 
maximum of 100 high-end automated test equipment (ATE) platforms.

setups and eliminating idle downtime associated with frequent load-board changeovers, 
Ardentec is able to achieve industry-leading throughput, uncompromised test cell

Proprietary accelerator roadmaps across cloud service providers (CSP) – including AWS 
Trainium/Inferentia, Meta MTIA, and Microsoft Maia – feature starkly heterogeneous probe 
card architectures, thermal docking requirements, and test firmware protocols. We believe 
the modular, multi-story footprint at the company’s Longtan facility provides the physical 
isolation necessary to construct dedicated, IP-secure production cleanrooms for multiple 
hyperscaler customers. As custom AI ASIC testing accounts for a rapidly expanding share 
of the company’s revenue mix relative to legacy automotive exposure, we believe this 
structural diversification serves as a strong catalyst for a rerating for Ardentec towards tier-
1 compute OSAT multiples.

Broad-based UTR inflection on peer capacity spillover 
While automotive and consumer segments navigate cyclical normalisation, Ardentec’s non-
AI capacity is staging a powerful recovery, per management. Based on our channel 
checks, its key OSAT peers (eg, KYEC: 2449 TT, not rated) have aggressively reallocated 
leading-edge test fleets toward mega-cap merchant AI GPUs, creating severe capacity 
bottlenecks for mobile application processor (AP), radio frequency (RF), and networking IC 
designers (such as MediaTek – 2454 TT, TWD4,625, Buy [1], covered by Rick Hsu). 
Therefore, Ardentec is capturing these high-volume, displaced wafer sorting (or chip 
probing, CP) and final test (FT) allocations. This order influx swiftly rebounds mature and 
communication line utilisation rates from trough levels (below 65%) back toward the 80%+ 
threshold, turning legacy capacity into a high-cash-generation baseline with zero asset 
drag.

Semiconductor testing carries a high fixed-cost structure heavily weighted toward 
equipment depreciation and engineering overhead. Historically, once aggregate corporate 
UTR crosses the critical 65% fixed-cost absorption threshold, incremental revenue drops 
directly to the bottom line with minimal variable costs (primarily power and consumables).

Next-gen CPO and silicon photonics testing 
Beyond the immediate custom ASIC cycle, Ardentec is securing its next structural growth 
frontier through advanced co-packaged optics (CPO) and silicon photonics (SiPh) wafer-
level testing. As hyperscale AI clusters push copper interconnects to physical transmission 
limits, the industry is transitioning towards optical I/O, which requires testing to shift from 
purely electrical verification to complex electro-optical (E/O) wafer sorting (CP). Unlike 
standard digital logic testing, optical probing demands sub-micron automated fibre 
alignment, precise laser wavelength calibration, optical power loss measurement, and 
specialised thermal-stability testing across photonic integrated circuits (PIC) and electronic 
integrated circuits (EIC) before advanced heterogeneous integration.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Market Analysis 
Structural shift in the IC testing industry 
Reiterating the key message in our Taiwan Testing Interface Initiation: paradigm shift 
accelerated by AI report, we believe the semiconductor testing paradigm is shifting. In the 
multi-die and 2.5D/3D packaging era (eg, TSMC CoWoS, SoIC), wafer testing (chip 
probing, CP) is no longer a commoditised, post-fab defect screening step – it has evolved 
into a mission-critical gating process. We summarise the critical paradigm shift as follows:

• The known good die (KGD) imperative. Heterogeneous integration bundles high-
value compute dies with multiple high bandwidth memory (HBM) stacks. Packaging an 
untested or marginally defective die risks scrapping the entire multi-thousand-dollar 
subsystem. This zero-tolerance threshold elevates CP from sample-based checks to 
exhaustive, full-spec validation across extreme thermal ranges.

• Structural Test-Time Inflation (2-3x). High reticle-limit AI/HPC custom silicon, 
expanding power envelopes (>700W), and thousands of high-speed interconnect 
channels have doubled or tripled test times per wafer. Consequently, automated test 
equipment (ATE) fleet demand is decoupling from raw silicon unit growth, driven instead 
by compounding tester hours.

• Next-Gen CPO Frontier. Rapid silicon tape-out cadences demand high-density vertical 
probe cards (VPC) and micro-cantilever interfaces supporting parallel multi-site testing. 
Beyond electrical testing, the emergence of CPO and SiPh requires active sub-micron 
optical alignment and electro-optical (E/O) sorting, creating multi-year technical barriers 
and pricing premiums for early movers.

Market dynamics: testing outperforms packaging 
According to Gartner (Semiconductor Assembly and Test Services, Worldwide, Sep 2025, 
Semiconductors and Electronics Team), total package and test market size is projected to 
increase from USD47.3bn to USD65.2bn over 2025-29E, representing an 8.4% CAGR 
over the period. Given that third-party market data typically blends packaging and testing 
into total OSAT, we granularly disaggregate historical revenue across major US and Taiwan 
vendors.

While consumer electronics endured a prolonged post-pandemic inventory correction over 
2022-25, the emergence of AI/HPC workloads created a pronounced divergence across 
the backend: historical industry data indicates that the testing sub-segment delivered 
materially higher year-over-year revenue growth than packaging over 2023-25, propelled 
by multi-die KGD mandates and structural test-time inflation.

Advantage of pure-play testing 
We think three reasons distinguish dedicated testing pure-plays from traditional integrated 
OSATs:

• Asset-light model and lean working capital mechanics. Integrated OSATs face 
substantial working capital exposure to expensive ABF substrates, precious metal 
bonding wires, and carrier materials. In contrast, pure-play testing carries virtually zero 
direct material inventory, insulating balance sheets from write-down risks. Furthermore, 
automated test equipment depreciation is concentrated directly within COGS, with 
capital intensity effectively moderated by customer-consigned tester allocations.

Global OSAT: Capex intensity

Source: Bloomberg

• Fleet flexibility and potential spillover orders. Unlike specialised packaging lines that 
require extensive physical retooling, standardised high-end ATE platforms exhibit 
exceptional fleet flexibility. Once test programs and interface hardware are qualified, 
testers can rapidly transition across diverse application domains – from premium mobile 
APs and automotive MCUs to complex custom ASICs – by swapping application-
specific load boards and high-density probe cards. As tier-1 OSATs concentrate their 
tester fleets on merchant AI GPU allocations, mobile AP, 5G RF, and high-speed 
networking chipmakers face severe capacity displacement. We believe pure-play test 
houses could benefit from a dual growth driver: rising demand for high-margin AI/HPC 
programs and incremental mobile and networking orders redirected to underutilised 
legacy tester capacity. This dynamic could decisively shift commercial bargaining 
leverage to pure testers, expanding hourly billing rates and unlocking structural gross 
margin leverage.

• IP neutrality and ecosystem balancing. North American hyperscalers designing 
proprietary custom silicon actively avoid monolithic vendor lock-in and potential IP 
conflicts within integrated supply chains. Pure-play testers provide a strictly neutral

deliver the physical segregation necessary to enforce proprietary security protocols and 
run dedicated engineering cells, allowing pure-play test houses to function as 
indispensable, IP-secure manufacturing partners across the advanced foundry 
ecosystem.

Financial analysis 
Revenue growth in 2026-28E 
We forecast Ardentec’s top-line to enter a multi-year expansion cycle, accelerating from 
TWD14.0bn in 2025 to TWD19.5bn in 2026E (+39.0% YoY), TWD26.8bn in 2027E 
(+37.5% YoY), and reaching TWD34.4bn in 2028E (+28.2% YoY). This represents a 3-year 
revenue CAGR of +34.8% (2025-28E), driven by a powerful confluence of volume 
expansion and structural pricing power across both advanced and mature test fleets.

First, we believe the orderly floor-by-floor commissioning of the 7-story Longtan facility 
delivers a predictable volume baseline, with each fully loaded cleanroom floor contributing 
c.TWD150m per month (c.TWD450m+ quarterly) in incremental run-rate revenue. Beyond 
pure wafer throughput, we expect revenue quality to see a significant mix upgrade, as 
advanced wafer sorting for hyperscaler AI ASICs and complex HPC processors command 
an hourly billing rate of nearly double (95-100% premium) that of traditional automotive and 
industrial IC testing, fundamentally elevating revenue density per tester hour.

Furthermore, as Ardentec prioritises engineering bandwidth, cleanroom footprint, and 
tester allocations toward high-margin advanced CP, legacy and mature-node test capacity 
faces internal supply tightness. This self-induced capacity crowding shifts pricing leverage 
back to Ardentec, allowing the company to enact upward price adjustments across lagging-
node test services and accelerating a broad-based, corporate-wide blended ASP 
expansion.

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Gross margin inflection driven by UTR surge  
We believe capacity utilisation rate serves as the single most critical determinant of 
Ardentec’s gross margin trajectory. Over the coming quarters, we project a structural 
rebound in corporate-wide utilisation driven by 2 powerful operational forces.

First, the rapid ramp of the Longtan advanced testing fab—operating at an optimal 85-90% 
UTR ceiling—will represent a rapidly expanding proportion of total capacity, immediately 
enriching the blended margin profile. Second, incoming spillover orders from tier-1 
communications and mobile AP clients are directly refilling legacy and mature testing lines. 
Because these mature tools are almost or already fully depreciated, incremental spillover 
revenue incurs virtually zero depreciation burden, generating exceptionally high operating 
leverage and outsized gross profit contribution, in our view.

Therefore, we estimate that this dual dynamic of high-end capacity scaling and fully 
amortised tool revitalisation will propel gross margins from 36.3% in 2025 to 42.3% in 
2026E, 44.5% in 2027E and reach 45.0% in 2028E.

Ardentec: gross margin

Source: Company, Daiwa forecasts

Earnings led by gross margin amid subdued operating 
leverage 
We believe that Ardentec’s earnings inflection is predominantly a story of gross margin and 
operating leverage rather than structural opex dilution. The high-fixed-cost nature of the 
wafer testing business—governed by multi-billion-dollar ATE depreciation and cleanroom 
overhead—is heavily concentrated within Cost of Goods Sold (COGS), per management. 
Consequently, the powerful fixed-cost absorption resulting from Longtan’s ramp and peer 
spillover orders directly fuels gross margin expansion, while operating leverage at the 
SG&A/opex level remains comparatively subdued.

Because high-end ASIC and electro-optical testing require tailored test program 
development, application-specific load board debugging and dedicated test engineering 
headcount for each CSP roadmap, we believe R&D and engineering talent function as 
quasi-variable expenses that scale in tandem with incremental floor additions. As a result, 
we project Ardentec’s opex-to-sales ratio to remain relatively stable in the 9-10% corridor 
over 2026-28E.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
While management has not provided official guidance due to dynamic cleanroom 
construction milestones and equipment lead-time variations, we model an orderly 
deployment cadence of one cleanroom floor per quarter at the Longtan mega-facility. 
Under this baseline, we forecast total capital expenditures (capex) to step up aggressively 
to TWD14.5bn in 2026E and TWD18.5bn in 2027E to support facility buildout and early tool 
installations.

To fund this expansion phase, Ardentec intends to prioritise low-cost bank borrowings over 
issuing overseas or domestic convertible bonds (ECB/CB), deliberately avoiding equity 
dilution to maximise EPS accretion during the upcoming earnings supercycle. 
Concurrently, management plans to optimise capital allocation by pragmatically calibrating 
its historical cash dividend pay-out ratio. We estimate the pay-out ratio will moderate 
slightly from 70% to 50% to retain internal cash for high-return AI capacity. Meanwhile, we 
believe the company actively negotiates customer-consigned tester models for subsequent 
cleanroom floors. This is a framework that insulates the cost structure from severe, front-
loaded depreciation burdens and channels capital directly into superior return on equity 
(ROE) growth.

Benefitting from disciplined financing, high asset turnover on advanced wafer sort lines and 
gross margin expansion, we project Ardentec's ROE to stage a structural recovery from the 
cyclical trough experienced during the 2023 automotive downturn, steadily increasing from 
15.1% to 32.1% over 2025-2027E.

Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
Given the current structural mix transition from cyclical automotives towards high-margin 
AI/HPC custom ASICs, amplified gross margin and operating leverage from the Longtan 
ramp, and ROE inflection likely to surpass its 2022 historical peak of c.23% over 2026-
28E, we initiate coverage of Ardentec with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD340, 
based on a target PER of 25.0x applied to our 4-quarter-forward EPS. Our target multiple 
of 25.0x is supported by a 48% EPS CAGR over 2025-28E (PEG of c.0.52x).

Historically, Ardentec traded within a compressed band of 10-25x forward PER due to its 
cyclical automotive and industrial exposure. While the stock has begun to re-rate alongside 
recent EPS upgrades, we view this multiple expansion as far from complete. In addition, 
we believe Ardentec still trades at a noticeable discount (14.9x PER on 2027E EPS) vs. 
leading AI testing peers (KYEC trading at 18.7x PER, while ASE at 26.4x), leaving ample 
room for multiple expansion as its pure-play test economics and earnings growth outpace 
the broader backend sector.

Risks to our call 
1. Delays in capacity expansion (primary risk). Delays in Longtan cleanroom and 
equipment installations, or customer qualifications could disrupt the floor-by-floor ramp, 
deferring high-margin hyperscaler ASIC revenue and diluting near-term gross margin 
leverage.  
2. Pricing pressure as a second-source supplier (secondary risk). Serving as a 
second-source supplier forces Ardentec to accept lower hourly rates than primary 
competitors. Furthermore, non-AI clients demand annual price cuts, limiting gross 
margin recovery unless capacity utilisation remains exceptionally high.  
3. Slow automotive and industrial recovery (secondary risk). Prolonged inventory 
adjustments in automotive and industrial markets continue to drag down IDM revenue 
share. Clients are placing short-term urgent orders rather than rebuilding long-term 
inventory, creating demand uncertainty for mature testing capacity.

### Page 18

18

Ardentec (3264 TT): 9 September 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Founded in 1999 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Ardentec is an independent 
semiconductor testing service provider. The company specialises in wafer testing (wafer 
sort) and final testing for memory, logic and mixed-signal integrated circuits. Its diverse 
portfolio addresses several sectors, including automotive electronics, 5G communications, 
storage and advanced AI/HPC ASICs.

Through strategic investments in Giga Solution and Raytek, Ardentec has also successfully 
expanded into RF testing and bumping capabilities. Its global customer base spans world-
class foundries, IDMs and fabless designers, such as MediaTek, Marvell, Texas 
Instruments, Infineon and NXP.

Ardentec’s core competitive advantage lies in its early leadership in automotive 
semiconductor testing, underpinned by ISO 26262 certification and stringent reliability 
standards that support strong customer stickiness and order stability. As an independent, 
pure-play testing provider, Ardentec serves as a neutral strategic partner to leading fabless 
companies, offering foundry-grade engineering capabilities, dedicated capacity and greater 
commercial flexibility.

Its role extends beyond that of a conventional second-source supplier: by providing an 
independent alternative to increasingly consolidated foundry and OSAT ecosystems, 
Ardentec helps customers diversify supply-chain risk, secure critical testing capacity and 
avoid potential bottlenecks. Backed by its state-of-the-art Longtan facility dedicated to 
advanced AI ASIC testing and a global manufacturing footprint across Taiwan, Nanjing, 
South Korea and Singapore, Ardentec is well positioned as an indispensable testing 
partner for tier-1 and emerging semiconductor customers.

Shareholder structure 
Ardentec’s shareholder base is well diversified, with no single controlling shareholder. 
Dresdner Bank AG is the largest holder with an 8.36% stake, followed by Macronix 
International (7.33%) and Vanguard Group (4.66%). The register also includes long-term 
institutional investors such as BlackRock, Dimensional Fund Advisors, BNP Paribas and 
Taiwan’s pension-related funds, providing a stable ownership structure and supporting 
governance transparency.

營 運 分 析 
華星光由 InP晶片延伸至光模組與高速光學封裝 
華星光（4979）成立於 2001 年，主要從事光通訊主動元件、InP（Indium 
Phosphide，磷化銦）晶片、光學次模組及高速光收發模組製造，製程涵蓋
Wafer Fab、Die Fab、Packaging，以及模組 Assembly與 Testing，具備由
晶片製造延伸至模組組裝測試的垂直整合能力。InP產品以 CW Laser、EML
及 APD為主，模組端則涵蓋 100G、400G 及 800G DCI 產品，並持續開發
1.6T。 
目前模組及封裝為主要營收來源，約占 8 成，InP 晶片約占 2 成。DCI 模組
維持單一主要客戶模式，雙方合作已14年且目前仍為獨家供應；InP晶片則
自 2026 年起增加客戶與產品種類，除既有CW Laser 外，EML及 APD亦陸
續進入量產。公司目前員工約 700 人，截至 26Q2 資本額約 14億元。  
近年營運重心由既有 DCI 模組代工及 CW Laser，逐步往 800G D CI、高速
EML、多波長與高功率 CW Laser，以及 NPO 光學封裝延伸。晶圓尺寸同步
由 2吋往 3吋、4吋移轉，並規劃4 至 6吋自動化產線；封裝端則已投入1.6T 
NPO製程及量產設備開發，預計27H2 進入量產，後續技術路線再往更高速
NPO及 CPO 延伸。

個股報告 
26Q2 認列時點差異壓低毛利率，26H2新品放量推升營收及獲利  
26Q2 營收 12.89 億元 （QoQ +8.0%、YoY +18.5%） ，毛利率22.4%，較26Q1
下降 3.0個百分點，營業利益 2.36 億元，稅後淨利1.84 億元，EPS 1.29元。
毛利率季減主要受到一筆財稅認列時點差異跨季影響，相關項目延至 26Q3
認列，目前未揭露該項目的金額及具體會計科目。26H1 累計營收 24.83 億
元、YoY +15.2%，稅後淨利 4.14 億元、YoY +34.4%，EPS 2.91元。 
26Q2 稅負亦高於正常水準。26H1 所得稅費用 1.21 億元，正常稅率以 20%
為基礎，惟 6 月另有未分配盈餘相關所得稅，使 26Q2 單季實質所得稅率升
至約 25.6%；研究部於 26H2及 2027 年回歸 20%正常化稅率估算。 
7月營收 5.09 億元、YoY +31.0%，創單月新高，稅前淨利 1.56 億元、稅後
淨利 1.31 億元，EPS 0.92元；前 7月累計營收 29.91 億元、YoY +17.6%，
前 7月 EPS約 3.83 元。7月獲利已開始反映 26H2 新品出貨增加。

26H2 營收增量主要來自 800G 及其他 DCI 新產品、既有 DCI、CW Laser
與 EML，其中 EML 已於 26H2 開始量產。研究部預估 26Q3 營收 16.31 億
元（QoQ +26.5%、YoY +51.5%） ，除前述跨季項目於Q3 認列外，隨新品
出貨及產能利用率提高，毛利率回升至 29.0%，EPS 2.33 元；26Q4 營收進
一步增至 19.05 億元 （QoQ +16.8%、YoY +65.0%） ， 毛利率28.6%，EPS 2.77
元。研究部預估 26H2 營收 35.36 億元，較 26H1 增加約 42%，與下半年新
品集中放量的出貨節奏一致。 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 60.18 億元、YoY +37.2%，毛利率26.8%、較 2025
年增加 5.8 個百分點，營業利益 13.78 億元、營業利益率 22.9%，稅後淨利
11.39億元、YoY +49.1%，EPS 8.0 元。全年毛利率提升主要反映 26H2高
速 DCI、CW Laser 及 EML出貨增加，以及營收規模放大後產能利用率改善；
26Q3 另有跨季認列項目回補，因此單季毛利率略高於 26Q4。

AI 資料中心規格升級，800G DCI於 2027年進入主要放量期 
資料中心高速互連需求持續上修，法說引用 LightCounting 資料顯示，2031
年 Intra-Data Center 市場規模預估由約 520 億美元提高至 800 億美元 。 整體
800G 光收發模組市場 2026 年需求已由先前預估約 3,500 萬支提高至約
7,000 萬支，1.6T 則由 2026 年約 1,500 萬支增至 2027年約 6,000 萬支，產
品規格持續往更高速、單模、多波長及高功率演進。 
DCI方面，2026 年仍以 400G 為主流，800G 已開始批量生產，2027 年進入
主要放量階段。法說引用市場資料估 2027 年 400G 與 800G DCI 合計需求
接近 200 萬支，其中800G 接近 100 萬支，2028 年增加至約 130 萬支、2029
年約 150 萬支；400G 於 2026 至 2027 年大致持平，2028 年後逐步下降。

華星光 DCI 100G、400G 與 800G 均已進入量產，800G 於 2026 年開始批
量出貨，2027 年客戶需求高於先前規劃，後續限制由訂單轉為供貨能力。依
目前產品出貨規劃，2027 年新增 DCI 產品營收可接近目前既有 DCI 業務規
模，既有產品同步成長下，整體 DCI 營收具一倍以上成長空間；2028 年新

- 4 - 
個股報告 
品營收規模則進一步擴大至目前既有產品的數倍，目前訂單能見度延伸至
2028 年底。

CW Laser 與 EML同步擴產，InP產能由 2吋往 3吋、4吋升級 
InP 產品線由 CW Laser、EML及 APD構成。CW Laser 目前已具 16 個波
長供應能力，相較一般單波或 4 波產品可覆蓋更多波長組合，多波長產品亦
具較佳毛利結構；EML 於 26H2開始量產，產品規格由100G 往 200G 升級，
主要對應高速、高功率及長距離單模應用；高增益APD亦於 26H2 進入量產
導入，應用包含車載光通訊。市場資料預估 2026 年 CW Laser 整體晶片需
求約 4億顆，EML相關需求約 1.6 億通道。

產能方面，CW Laser 與 EML 後段製程同步擴充，不因 EML 放量排擠 CW 
Laser。 雷射晶片月產能預計由2026年底約 6KK顆提高至 2027年底約 12KK
顆，2027 年底較 2026 年底增加一倍。 
晶圓尺寸同步由 2吋往 3吋、4吋移轉，2027 年以 3吋為主要生產平台，再
逐步增加 4 吋比重；4 至 6 吋自動化設備已進入規劃，預計 27Q4 起陸續到
位。產品規格方面，CW Laser 由 70／100mW 往更高功率延伸，並布局
400mW 搭配 SOA產品，EML則由 100G 往 200G 升級。晶圓尺寸放大、製
程自動化與產品規格提升共同支撐 2027 年 InP產量增加。

NPO於 27H2量產，高精度封裝能力往 CPO延伸 
華星光在既有 InP雷射、光元件封裝與DCI模組基礎上，進一步布局NPO。
1.6T NPO 目前進行封裝製程及量產設備開發，並與客戶及台、日設備廠共
同開發，預計27Q2 前完成開發、27H2 進入量產，2028 年開始較大量貢獻；
3.2T NPO 亦已展開前期開發，中長期技術路線再延伸至 CPO。 
NPO端已完成 Fiber Array 及 Laser Array 貼合 prototype，量產設備仍與客
戶及設備廠共同開發；Active Alignment 精度可進入 0.1μm 以下，封裝能力
已由一般光元件組裝往高密度光學引擎所需的高精度貼合延伸。 
27H2 NPO量產對 2027 年財測的直接營收貢獻仍有限，較大量貢獻預計落
在 2028 年；2027 年營收與獲利成長仍主要由 800G 及新增 DCI產品、CW 
Laser 與 EML擴產帶動，NPO則為 2028 年後新增成長來源。

廠區進度逐步明確，2027年 CAPEX超過 30億元 
產能擴張由不同廠區分別承接。合江廠逐步集中 InP Fab，規劃將既有空間
轉為晶圓製造用途，長期目標月產能約 3,000 片，後續再導入 4至 6吋自動
化產線；八德廠主要承接 DCI 模組 Assembly 與 Testing，並規劃保稅工廠
功能；吉林廠及新增空間則以 Packaging 與 NPO 相關產品為主，使 InP、
DCI與新封裝產品逐步形成獨立的產能配置。 
無塵室面積自 2024年底約 4,000平方公尺持續擴充 ，2025年新增合江廠 950

平方公尺及錦鼎廠 1,500 平方公尺，2026 年再增加約 1,700 平方公尺，至

底完成約 5,500 平方公尺無塵室建置 ， 屆時全公司無塵室面積約13,650 平方
公尺；2028 年另規劃利用八德廠剩餘容積再新增約 10,000 平方公尺。 
2026 年 CAPEX 實際現金支出預估 18 至 21 億元，不包含已發包但尚未付
款部分，主要投入 InP Fab、模組生產、廠房與無塵室；2027 年 CAPEX保
守估計超過 30億元，延續廠房、無塵室及設備擴充，2028年再進行八德廠
後續土建。

圖二、華星光(4979)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

800G 與 InP產能擴張支撐 2027年成長，佈局 NPO/CPO未來展望正向 
研究部預估華星光 2026 年營收 60.2 億元、YoY +37.2%，毛利率 26.8%、
年增 5.8個百分點，營業利益13.8 億元，營業利益率22.9%，稅後淨利 11.4
億元、YoY +49.1%，EPS 8.0 元。26H2 隨 800G 及其他 DCI新產品、既有
DCI、CW Laser 與 EML出貨增加，預估營收及毛利率均較 26H1 提升，其
中 26Q3 另有前季跨季認列項目於本季反映。 
2027 年 800G DCI 進入主要放量期，新增 DCI 產品營收規模預計接近目前
既有 DCI產品， 加上既有產品持續成長 ；InP 端 CW Laser 與 EML同步擴產，
雷射晶片月產能由 2026 年底約 6KK 顆逐步提升至 2027 年底約 12KK 顆，
並提高 3吋晶圓生產比重 。 研究部預估2027年營收 92.6億元 、YoY +53.9%，
毛利率 28.7%、年增 1.9 個百分點，營業利益 23.4 億元、YoY +69.7%，營
業利益率 25.2%， 稅後淨利19.2 億元 、YoY +68.1%，EPS 13.5元。1.6T NPO
預計 27H2 量產，初期營收貢獻有限，較明顯貢獻預計自 2028 年開始。

- 1 - 
個股報告

聯茂(6213) 
形勢正好，營運轉佳 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $757 
前次投資建議：無

報告日期：2026.09.09 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 玻纖布供給釋出，稼動率有望回升，2026 年 EPS 19.1元 
➢ 未來三年持續擴產，漲價與產品組合轉佳，將帶動利潤顯著提升，2027 年 EPS 30.3 元，考
量未來趨勢明確且 H2 漲價效益持續顯現，帶動公司營運體質有顯著提升，兆豐投顧給予目
標價 757 元(25X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 48.4%，投資評等為「買進」 。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/9/8 收盤價： 510.00 ➢ 聯茂 (6213)公告 8月營收：營收 50.4億元， MoM 
+6.2%/YoY +97.9% ，再創單月新高，兆豐投顧上修
26Q3營收至147.6億元，QoQ +28%，係因通用型伺服
器新平台放量帶動高階材料占比同步墊高，同時原物料
緊缺仍有漲價空間，預期在產品組合改善與報價上漲帶
動毛利率提升至 28%，季增6ppt，同步上修稅後淨利
16.6億元，QoQ +29.8%/YoY +411.5%，EPS 4.4元。 
➢ 展望2026年，隨著原物料供需吃緊與成本上升，公司
將持續調漲報價因應，同時預計於26Q4玻纖布供給有
部分改善，有助於稼動率提升，在漲價效益完整反映與
產品組合逐步改善下，預估聯茂 2026年營收 516.7億
元，YoY +56.1% ，毛利率24.1%，年增9.6ppt，稅後
淨利69.3億元，YoY +359%，EPS 19.1元。 
➢ 展望後市，聯茂持續在未來三年擴產，產能將由今年底
570萬張提升至2029年910萬張，增加約60%，主要鎖
定AI Server與低軌衛星需求；此外美系GPU客戶M9材
料已通過認證，預期 2027年開始小量出貨，推升公司
高階料號占比持續墊高，加上新產能開出，將使產品組
合轉佳。綜上所述，兆豐投顧預估聯茂 2027年營收
683.8億元，YoY +32.4%，毛利率29.9%，年增5.8ppt，
稅後淨利110.1億元，YoY +58.8%，EPS 30.3元，考
量未來趨勢明確且H2漲價效益持續顯現，帶動公司營
運體質有顯著提升，兆豐投顧給予目標價 757元(25X 
27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間48.4%，投資評等為 「買進」 。

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

聯茂為國內第二大 CCL供應商  
聯茂(6213)成立於 1997 年，2008 年掛牌上市，總部位於新竹縣新埔鎮，最
大股東為半導體廠穩懋。公司為全球前十大銅箔基板 (CCL)廠、國內第二大
CCL供應商，同時是通用型伺服器CCL的主力供應商，在AMD平台市占率
逾六成、Intel 平台約三至四成。主力產品涵蓋銅箔基板(CCL)、玻纖布膠片
(Prepreg)、多層壓合基板及軟性基材，近年並將觸角延伸至 HDI 板材、IC
載板用材料與散熱材料，積極卡位 AI 伺服器、低軌衛星地面設備等高階應
用，作為公司下一階段成長動能。

圖一、全球特殊基板供應商市佔率

- 3 - 
個股報告 
生產基地涵蓋台灣新竹，中國江西、無錫、東莞、廣州以及泰國廠。銅箔基
板月產能於 2025 年底約 540 萬張，隨新產能陸續開出，預計 2026 年底增
至約 570 萬張，並規劃 2027 年底達 630 萬張、2028 年底 825 萬張、2029
年底逾 900 萬張，新增產能主要鎖定 M7 以上高階料號以因應 AI 伺服器需
求。2Q26 應用別營收結構以基礎建設(通用及 AI伺服器、網通設備等)為主，
占比約 73%，其次為車用電子 14%、消費性電子 8%與智慧型手機 5%。

- 4 - 
個股報告 
原物料況仍吃緊，CCL報價將持續上揚，26Q3 EPS 4.4元 
聯茂(6213)公告 8 月營收：營收 50.4 億元，MoM + 6.2%/YoY +97.9%，再
創單月新高，兆豐投顧上修 26Q3 營收至 147.6 億元，QoQ +28%/YoY 
+91.1%，係因通用型伺服器新平台放量帶動高階材料占比同步墊高，同時原
物料緊缺仍有漲價空間，預期在產品組合改善與報價上漲帶動毛利率提升至
28%，季增6ppt，同步上修稅後淨利 16.6 億元 ，QoQ +29.8%/YoY +411.5%，
EPS 4.4元。

玻纖布供給釋出，稼動率有望回升，2026年 EPS 19.1元 
展望 2026 年，隨著原物料供需吃緊與成本上升，公司將持續調漲報價因應，
同時預計於 26Q4 玻纖布供給有部分改善，有助於稼動率提升，在漲價效益
完整反映與產品組合逐步改善下，預估聯茂 2026 年營收 516.7 億元，YoY 
+56.1%，毛利率 24.1%，年增 9.6ppt，稅後淨利 69.3 億元，YoY +359%，
EPS 19.1元。

未來三年持續擴產，漲價與產品組合轉佳，將帶動利潤顯著提升，2027 年
EPS 30.3 元 
展望後市，預期 CCL/PCB需求將延至 2029 年，係因 AI Server PCB層數大
幅提升，且在新平台啟動帶動 M6 以上高階材料需求進一步擴大，有利整體
ASP提升，輔以漲價帶動營收與利潤持續改善，同時玻纖布產能供應持續釋
放，將有助稼動率提升，聯茂持續在未來三年擴產，產能將由今年底 570 萬
張進一步提升至 2029 年 910 萬張，產能增加約 60%，主要鎖定 AI Server
與低軌衛星需求；此外美系 GPU 客戶 M9 材料已通過認證，預期 2027 年開
始小量出貨，推升公司高階料號占比持續墊高，加上無錫廠新產能開出，將
使產品組合轉佳。綜上所述，兆豐投顧預估聯茂 2027 年營收 683.8 億元，
YoY +32.4% ，毛利率 29.9%，年增 5.8ppt，稅後淨利 110.1 億元，YoY 
+58.8%，EPS 30.3元。

期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。 
本刊所刊載之內容僅做為參考，惟已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量
投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本 報告內容必須經本公司同意。

安葆(7792) 
買進, TP 410 (潛在報酬 33.12%)  
買進 
Price(09/02):308 
Target Price：410

公司受惠海內外半導體業擴廠需求強勁，截至2Q26 在手訂單已達
145.5億元，若加計LOI等潛在案源，總金額上看200億元，且能
見度已延伸至 2028 年，短中期成長動能明確。中長期則受惠於
AIDC加速建置，可望成為下一波主要成長動能，目前洽談中的相
關需求已達 20GW，若以 150~180萬美元/MW預估，市場規模相
當可觀，未來將持續拓展美國、德國等海外市場，現階段則以與鴻
海合作之 100MW 資料中心為主要進展，預計於 4Q26～1Q27 交
貨。獲利方面，隨海外訂單比重提升 30%，加上 2H25 起調漲新
訂單價格，並透過大規模、提前採購降低物料成本及供應風險，整
體獲利能力仍具上修空間。展望後市，公司致力由電力設備供應商
轉型為機電整合廠商，目前已取得美國GC總承包、電氣及機械承
包商資格，隨工程業務逐步放量，將擴大對營收之貢獻。綜合以上，
研究部預估 2026、2027 年 EPS 20.14、33.09元，以2H26~1H27 
EPS*15 倍 PER，給予目標價 410元，投資建議為買進。 
 近期投資評等  海外營收比重提升，帶動 2Q26毛利率創高 
2Q26 營收 18.1 億元，YoY+14.3%，QoQ+32.41%，稅後損
益 2.38 億元，YoY+168.55%，QoQ+204.67%，EPS 5.1 元，
優於研究部預期。營收結構方面，機組及工程安裝52%、UPS
不斷電系統 34%、維修保養 7%、其他 7%。受惠於半導體客
戶海內外擴廠積極，電力設備需求強勁，帶動2Q26 營收呈雙
位數年增季增。獲利方面， 2Q26 毛利率 23.16%，
YoY+11.97ppt，QoQ+8.03ppt，創歷史新高，因海外訂單價
格及付款條件均優於國內 ， 且海外營收比重已由前一季的17%
提升至 19%；此外，公司自 2H25 起調漲新訂單價格，加上
主要客戶調整發包模式，由單廠單次發包改為三廠同時發包，
公司得以透過大規模及提前採購降低物料成本與供應風險，進
一步提升整體獲利動能。

 半導體擴廠+AIDC加速建置，訂單能見度至 2028年 
展望 2026 年，受惠於半導體擴廠需求強勁，以及 AI 資料中
心加速佈建，整體用電需求顯著提升，近年公司積極將備援型
電力轉換為主用型電力，有效提升設備效率並降低客戶轉換成
本。隨主要客戶資本支出持續擴增，公司在手訂單創歷史新
高，截至2Q26 已達 145.5 億元，QoQ+32.3%；1H26 新接單

本資料內容僅供參考，投資人應審慎考量自身之投資需求與風險，本公司恕不負任何法律責任，亦不作任何保證 
第一金證券投資顧問股份有限公司(First Capital Management Inc.)台北市長安東路一段 35 號 3F   TEL(02) 2563-1666 
2
除息日 2026/06/17 量為 120 億元，為2025全年的兩倍，若加計LOI等潛在案源，
總金額上看 200 億元，訂單能見度則延伸至2028 年，且部分
產品交期已排至 2030 年。區域別來看，台灣佔比約56%、美
國則提升至 30%；產業別則以半導體為主，佔比約 90%，包
含 T公司台灣及美國廠、美光新加坡廠、華邦電、群創等。

隨 AI 資料中心快速擴張，電力供應及電網等基礎建設成為算
力擴張主要瓶頸，科技巨頭因此加速推動 Stargate 等大型基
礎建設計畫，目前公司洽談中的AIDC 相關需求已達 20GW，
規模約當是台灣每日發電量一半以上，若以 150~180 萬美元
/MW 預估，市場規模相當可觀。公司將以縮短產品交期及強
化工程服務能力為發展方向，並規劃成立 O&M 公司，提供效
率化的售後維運服務；目前相關設備已取得 UL、CE 認證，
未來將以美國工廠作為海外發貨中心，持續拓展美國、德國等
海外市場。現階段則以與鴻海合作之100MW 資料中心為主要
進展，預計於4Q26～1Q27 交貨，屆時將陸續反映於財報上。

產能方面，因應主要客戶及國際客戶擴廠計畫明確，公司規劃
於台灣成立製造中心，包含台南二期廠及屏東廠。台南二期廠
預計 9M26 動工、1Q27 完成建置，將以模組化預製產能為主，
生產海外電池櫃及預製排煙管，可有效簡化現場工序並降低人
力需求；屏東廠則預計 1Q27 動工、1Q28完工投產，主要生
產外銷配電盤、電池櫃等產品，且因位於屏科園區，可享有免
稅區出口等相關稅賦優惠。公司亦逐步由電力設備供應商轉型
為機電整合廠商，因主要客戶擴廠速度加快，帶動機電統包需
求增加，在供需失衡下，公司於6M26 已取得亞利桑那州及德
州 GC 總承包、電氣及機械承包商資格，預計年底將切入機電
工程包，進一步擴大業務範疇，帶動工程收入提升。

考量廠房建置期約 2~3 年，研究部預估 2026、2027 年機電
工程收入可維持雙位數年增，且隨海外訂單比重提升至30%，
並享有較佳訂單價格及付款條件，將進一步優化整體獲利結
構。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026 年營收 76.67 億元，
YoY+28.08%，稅後損益9.4 億元，YoY+152.43%，EPS 20.14
元；2027 年營收 106.81 億元，YoY+39.31%，稅後損益15.44
億元，YoY+64.32%，EPS 33.09 元。

## Document 166: 8月贸易数据点评：出口增速回升，AI支撑仍强

Source PDF: all report pdf/8月贸易数据点评：出口增速回升，AI支撑仍强.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

[Table_RightTitle] 
宏观研究|宏观跟踪报告

[Table_Title] 
出口增速回升，AI 支撑仍强

[Table_ReportType] 
——8月贸易数据点评

[Table_ReportDate] 2026年 09月 09 日

[T able_Summary] ⚫ 事件：按美元计价，8月出口当月同比25%，前值23.9%；进
口当月同比28.2%，前值27.5%，贸易顺差1190.85亿美元。 
⚫ 出口增速回升，海外制造业景气度仍高。台风天气扰动下，出
口订单并未回落，海外制造业景气度依然较高，韩国半导体出
口保持旺盛。AI产业链拉动仍强，关税预期和全球航运成本
上升影响下， “抢出口”效应也给出口带来一定推动。我国出
口集装箱运价持续走高，出口价格指数放缓。人民币仍处升值
通道，短期对出口影响较小。 
⚫ 对东盟、非洲出口保持旺盛，对美国出口持续修复，但中长期
贸易关系依然承压。8月对美出口上行幅度相对最大，低基数
起到一定抬升作用。7 月美国新一轮301关税落地，8 月美国
对华贸易税负增幅相对有限，依然聚焦AI算力、新能源等核
心产业链，未来仍需观测301 产能过剩调查是否实质性落地。
9 月1日G20峰会发布了“主席声明” ， 中国反对其中的四个
部分，分别涉及霍尔木兹海峡、全球贸易失衡、IMF监督机制
和主权债务重组。全球秩序仍处重构进程当中，未来外贸仍存
在一定不确定性。 
⚫ AI 相关产品出口旺盛，劳动密集型产品出口持续改善。集成
电路、自动数据处理设备出口金额同比持续走高，价格上涨为
主要驱动。下游汽车、船舶制造业出口放缓，但增速维持高
位。劳动密集型产品出口跌幅持续收窄，基数走低带来一定支
撑。原料端钢材、铝材、成品油出口上行，稀土高位回落。 
⚫ 进口增速反弹上行，价格支撑边际减弱。进口价格总指数由高
位回落，但原料端价格仍强，AI产品涨价也给进口带来强支
撑。AI产业链进出口均保持旺盛，农产品进口增速回落。能
源、金属材料进口表现分化，原油类、铜进口走弱，煤及褐
煤、钢材等增速上行。下游汽车消费品进口跌幅加深。 
⚫ 出口需求韧性有望持续，AI仍是重要支撑。随着台风天气扰
动消退，延迟发货的产品有望逐步释放，9 月出口有望保持强
势。海外需求整体表现较好，将给后续出口带来支撑。AI产
业发展将持续拉动出口，未来仍需关注半导体、数据处理设备
等产品的价格和库存水平；劳动密集型产品出口有望持续修
复。PPI年内高点已过，后续价格支撑或逐步减弱，随着“抢
出口”效应逐步释放，需求或边际下行。美国中期选举临近，
关税政策仍有一定调整风险。整体来看，四季度出口面临一定
不确定性，但仍有较强支撑，全年出口增速有望维持高位。 
⚫ 风险因素：海外政策收紧幅度超预期，中美贸易关系变动超预
期，地缘政治风险发展超预期。

产需景气度回暖，价格指标上行 
企业利润修复放缓，行业分化格局持续

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 3 页 共 8 页  
事件 
2026年8月， 按美元计价， 我国出口4014.41亿美元， 当月同比25%（市场预期21.9%），
前值23.9%，累计同比由18.5%继续上行至19.3%；进口金额2823.56亿美元，当月同比
28.2%（市场预期28.1%）， 前值27.6%； 贸易顺差1190.85亿美元， 前值1123.37亿美元，
贸易差额当月同比由15.4%上行至17.9%。按人民币计价，8月出口当月同比18.6%，上
月为17.8%，进口同比21.7%，上月为21.3%。

1    出口增速再度反弹，AI产业链拉动仍强 
出口增速反弹上行，外需支撑仍强，产业拉动特征持续。按美元计价，8月我国出口
4014.41亿美元，当月同比由7月的23.9%上行至25%，高于市场预期，增速延续高位，
累计同比由18.5%继续上行至19.3%。台风天气扰动下，出口订单并未回落。日本、欧
元区制造业PMI上行，美国制造业PMI高位小幅回落，海外制造业景气度依然较高。韩
国出口增速由63%上行至68.7%，半导体出口保持旺盛。美国7月制造业库存和新增订
单同比增速均上行，美国经济韧性较强。AI产业链拉动仍强，关税预期和全球航运成
本上升影响下， “抢出口”效应也给出口带来一定推动。 
保税物流方式进出口增速保持领跑，且上行幅度相对最大，民企、外企进出口增速均
上行，国企进出口增速回落。2026年前8个月，从贸易方式看，我国一般贸易进出口
同比由10.2%上行至10.5%（按人民币计价），加工贸易进出口同比由26.3%小幅上行至
26.4%，保税物流进出口同比由40.8%上行至42.3%。从外贸主体看，前8个月，民营企
业进出口同比由17.2%进一步上行至17.6%；外商投资企业进出口同比由17.6%上行至
18.1%；国有企业进出口同比由17.3%回落至16.9%。

持旺盛。对美国出口累计同比上行3.6个百分点至6.2%，上行幅度相对最大，低基数
起到一定抬升作用。7月美国新一轮301关税落地，8月美国对华贸易税负增幅相对有

限，依然聚焦AI算力、新能源等核心产业链，美国中期选举前夕贸易政策大幅调整可
能性较低，未来仍需观测301产能过剩调查是否实质性落地。9月1日美国北卡罗莱纳
州举行的G20峰会闭幕，会议未能通过联合声明，仅发布了“主席声明” ，中国反对其
中的四个部分，分别涉及霍尔木兹海峡、全球贸易失衡、IMF监督机制和主权债务重
组。声明认为：长期、过度的贸易顺差会给其他国家造成冲击，顺差过大的国家应取
消压制国内消费、过度依赖出口的非市场政策。我国强调各方应“全面、客观、平衡
看待全球失衡问题” 。全球秩序仍处重构进程当中，未来外贸仍存在一定不确定性。 
我国出口集装箱运价持续走高，出口价格指数放缓，进口价格指数回落。8月我国出
口集装箱运价指数高位继续攀升，已经接近2024年7月的高点。华东台风引发堵港，
霍尔木兹海峡航运持续受阻，红海危机再度发酵，导致大量船舶绕行好望角，推动运
价持续抬升。从已经公布的7月数据来看，我国出口价格总指数上行，矿产品出口价
格高位持续回落，化工相关产品出口价格持续抬升；进口价格指数边际回落。

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

人民币仍处升值通道，短期对出口影响较小。人民币在8月延续升值趋势，我国经济
表现稳健，产业结构优化升级带来竞争优势，出口保持旺盛，贸易顺差1190.85亿美
元，前值1123.37亿美元，贸易顺差环比持续扩张，企业结售汇意愿较强。近期美国
日本联合干预日元，日元兑美元升值，美元指数有所下跌。美联储加息预期上行，叠
加对美国债务问题的担忧，美元指数短期上行受阻。多因素交织作用下，支撑人民币
升值。我国对外出口市场多元化布局，产业链、价格优势明显，短期人民币升值对企
业出口的约束较小。

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

AI相关产品出口旺盛，汽车、船舶等制造业出口放缓，但增速维持高位，劳动密集型
产品出口持续改善，农产品出口表现分化。从出口商品来看，前8个月，我国机电产
品出口增速由21.2%进一步上行至21.9%， 仍是出口重要支撑项。AI产业链出口保持旺
盛，集成电路出口金额累计同比由99.5%继续攀升至103.9%， 自动数据处理设备由45.2%
上行至49.4%，价格上涨为主要驱动。下游制造业出口回落但仍具韧性，船舶出口由
37.9%回落至35.2%，汽车和汽车底盘由54.9%回落至53.2%，增速仍处高位，手机出口
增速进一步上行。劳动密集型产品出口跌幅持续收窄， 基数走低带来一定支撑， 鞋靴、
玩具、箱包等跌幅收窄，服饰、灯具、家具等增速保持上行。原料端出口大多上行，
地缘政治扰动带动原油价格上行，成品油出口增速本月上行幅度最大，钢材、铝材增
速上行，稀土高位边际回落。农产品方面，粮食出口增速反弹上行，水产品出口持平
于-0.5%；肥料出口自6月以来持续大幅回落，本月跌幅最深，主要受出口管控政策影
响。

2    进口增速上行，下游消费品进口依然偏弱 
进口增速反弹上行，价格支撑边际减弱。2026年8月，我国进口金额2823.56亿美元，
按美元计价，当月同比由7月的27.6%上行至28.2%，与市场预期28.1%接近；累计同比
由26.8%上行至27%，进口上行幅度放缓。按人民币计价，8月进口当月同比21.7%，上
月为21.3%。进口价格总指数由高位回落，商品价格对进口的支撑作用边际减弱，但
大宗商品价格同比持续走高，原料端价格仍强，AI产品涨价也给进口带来强支撑。我
国对美国进口增速持续修复，巴西、澳大利亚等资源国进口保持高位，AI产业链发展
强势的带动下，对韩国进口增速进一步上行至72.5%，对欧盟出口增速持续下滑。 
图表8：我国对不同国家进口增速分化（%） 图表9：大宗同比持续上行，进口增速反弹（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 
AI产业链进出口均保持旺盛，能源、金属材料进口表现分化，农产品进口增速回落。
从进口产品来看，前8个月，我国高技术产品进口累计同比由37.1%上行至40.1%；自
动数据处理设备增速上行11个百分点至96.5%，上行幅度相对最大，集成电路、二极
管及类似半导体器件增速均上行。汽车零件、汽车和汽车底盘进口跌幅加深，飞机进
口跌幅持续收窄。农产品进口整体回落，其中食用植物油跌幅较大为主要拖累，鲜、
干水果及坚果增速上行。能源类进口分化，受油价高位波动影响，原油、成品油增速
进一步回落，煤及褐煤增速保持上行，天然气进口跌幅持续修复。上游资源品进口也
表现分化，铜矿砂、铁矿砂、铜材进口增速回落，钢材、原木、纸浆、橡胶进口增速
修复上行。 
图表10：农产品、原油进口回落（%） 图表11：AI产业链进口金额增速持续上行（%）

3    总结与展望 
出口需求韧性有望持续，AI仍是重要支撑。海外需求整体表现较好，有望给后续出口
带来支撑。随着台风天气扰动消退，延迟发货的产品有望逐步释放，9月出口有望保
持强势。AI产业发展有望持续拉动出口，未来仍需关注半导体、数据处理设备等产品
的价格和库存水平；劳动密集型产品出口有望持续修复。PPI年内高点已过，后续价
格支撑或逐步减弱，随着“抢出口”效应逐步释放，需求或边际下行。美国中期选举
临近，关税政策仍有一定调整风险。整体来看，四季度出口虽然面临一定不确定性，
但仍有较强支撑，全年出口增速有望维持高位。

4    风险提示 
海外政策收紧幅度超预期，中美贸易关系变动超预期，地缘政治风险发展超预期。

2030년 판매 목표 555만대, xEV 비중 60% 목표 
현대차는 글로벌 판매 목표로 2030년 555만대를 제시했다. 이는 2025년 
410만대 대비 약 35% 증가한 수준으로, 글로벌 시장점유율 역시 4.7%에서 
6.0%까지 확대한다는 계획이다. 파워트레인별로는 전체 판매에서 xEV 비중
을 2025년 23%에서 2030년 60%까지 확대할 예정이다. 특히 수익성이 
높은 HEV 판매 확대를 통한 믹스 개선과 xEV 규모의 경제 확보를 바탕으로 
2030년 영업이익률 목표를 기존 8~9%에서 9%+로 상향했다. 북미에서는 
2030년까지 10종 이상의 HEV 라인업을 구축하고 HEV 판매 비중을 50%
까지 확대하며, 유럽 EV 판매 역시 2025년 11.6만대에서 2030년 42만대 
이상으로 확대할 계획이다. 여기에 Santa Fe EREV를 시작으로 2027년부터 
현대·제네시스·아이오닉 브랜드 전반에 EREV를 순차 적용할 예정이다. 
판매 확대는 특정 지역에 집중하기보다 전 권역 성장 전략을 통해 달성할 예
정이다. 2030년 판매 비중은 북미 26%, 유럽 15%, 인도 15%, 국내 13%, 
중국 9% 수준을 목표로 하고 있으며, 중국 판매는 2025년 13만대에서 
2030년 50만대 이상으로 확대할 계획이다. 향후 판매 증가분의 50% 이상
은 신규 세그먼트 및 신차 라인업에서 창출할 예정으로, 북미 HEV·SUV, 유
럽 EV, 인도 SUV, 중국 IONIQ 라인업을 중심으로 성장을 추진할 전망이다. 
신차 사이클 본격화, 2027년 EREV 출시 
향후 8개월간 All-New 아반떼, IONIQ 3, All -New Tucson 및 Tucson 
Hybrid, Santa Fe EREV 등 총 7종의 핵심 신차가 출시될 예정이다. 중장기
적으로는 2030년까지 완전변경·상품성 개선·신규 파워트레인을 포함해 100
종 이상의 신차를 출시하고, 18개 이상의 신규 모델 및 세그먼트에 진입한다
는 계획이다. 제네시스 역시 2026년 9월 GV80 Hybrid를 시작으로 첫 HEV 
라인업을 구축하고, 2027년 초에는 640마일 이상의 주행거리를 목표로 하는 
EREV를 출시할 예정이다.

Company Issue Brief / 현대차 / 2026. 8. 27 
LS Securities Research 2 
주주환원 정책 유지 
주주환원 정책은 기존 기조를 유지한다. 2025~2027년 총주주환원율 35% 이상을 목표로 최소 DPS 1
만원, 최소 배당성향 25% 이상, 분기 DPS 2,500원을 유지하며, 2026년에는 약 0.8조원 규모의 자사
주 251만주를 소각할 예정이다. 실적 변동성 확대에도 기존 밸류업 정책을 유지한다는 점에서 하방 밸
류에이션을 지지하는 요인으로 판단된다. 
로보틱스 산업화 본격화, 2028년 美 생산 개시 
로보틱스 사업은 기존 기술개발 중심에서 양산 및 상업화 단계로 진입할 전망이다. 현대차그룹은 2026
년 미국 RMAC(Robot Metaplant Application Center)를 개소하고 실제 공장 환경에서 데이터를 수집
하며 로봇 적용 검증을 진행하고 있다. 연말까지 RMAC 부지를 현재 대비 10배 확대하고, 5개 차종 67
개 작업을 대상으로 시퀀싱·고반복 작업·품질검사 등의 테스트를 진행할 예정이다. 이후 축적된 현장 데
이터를 기반으로 2028년 HMGMA에 로봇을 양산 배치하고 2030년부터 글로벌 생산거점으로 확대 적
용한다는 계획이다. 
동시에 미국 내 최초의 대규모 상업용 로봇 생산시설도 구축한다. 2026년 생산부지를 발표하고 2028
년부터 연간 3만대 규모의 로봇 생산을 시작할 예정이며, 현대차그룹이 우선적으로 2.5만대의 초도 물
량을 확보했다. 그룹 내부 Captive 수요를 기반으로 초기 양산 규모를 확보한 뒤 물류 및 산업 분야 외
부 고객으로 판매처를 확대한다는 전략이다. 이에 따라 Boston Dynamics는 단순 R&D 자산에서 그룹 
제조혁신과 외부 판매를 동시에 담당하는 사업으로 전환될 전망이다. 
AI 측면에서는 차량·자율주행·로보틱스 전반에서 발생하는 데이터를 하나의 Data Flywheel로 연결한다
는 전략이다. Gleo AI와 자율주행용 Atria AI를 중심으로 데이터를 지속 학습하고, 2029년부터는 새만금 
AI 데이터센터를 독자 운영할 예정이다. 데이터센터는 100MW, GPU 5만개 이상의 인프라를 갖출 계획
이며, Physical AI 데이터센터와 로봇 Reference Platform 구축을 통해 향후 로봇 및 자율주행 모델 학
습 속도를 높일 전망이다.

KEY DATA RELEASES AND EVENTS NEXT WEEK 
• BOK and BSP are set to hike rates, while BOT stays on an extended pause. 
• Singapore’s core and headline inflation are expected to tick up. 
• Hong Kong export growth to moderate.

The Week Ahead August 21, 2026

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank of Thailand (BOT) (26 August)

We expect the BOT to maintain its policy 
rate at 1.00% at its August meeting, 
extending the pause following June’s 
unanimous decision. Economic growth 
remains uneven, with both private 
consumption and foreign tourism weak 
but stabilising, while goods expor ts and 
private investment remain strong. 
Headline inflation, although elevated, 
has eased for three consecutive months, 
falling to 1.9% yoy in July from rates near 
the upper end of the BOT’s 1 -3% target 
range, largely due to lower energy prices. 
Given the uneven pace of economic 
growth and headline inflation remaining 
within the central bank’s target range, 
the BOT retains scope to keep monet ary 
policy unchanged and accommodative in 
order to support the economic recovery 
and complement fiscal policy amid 
ongoing geopolitical uncertainties.

Bank of Korea (27 August)

We expect the Bank of Korea to raise the 
base rate by a further 25bps to 3.00% at 
this meeting, alongside an upgrade to its 
annual macroeconomic forecasts. There 
is significant room for the BOK to revise 
up its 2026 GDP growth forecast to 
around 3.5%, from  the current 2.6%, 
given the stronger -than-expected 1H 
growth of 3.8% yoy. There is also room to 
revise up its 2027 CPI inflation forecast 
to close to 3.0%, from the current 2.3%.  
Although headline CPI moderated 
slightly to 2.8% yoy in July, from 3.2% in 
June, core CPI continued to edge up to 
2.6% from 2.5%, while housing prices 
also increased further, to 2.7% from 
2.6%. These developments should keep 
the BOK cautious about the risk of 
inflation remaining above its 2% target 
for an extended period.

A hawkish hold at this meeting cannot be 
ruled out, however. This view mainly 
reflects the recent tightening in financial 
market conditions, driven by strong KRW 
appreciation and heightened KOSPI 
volatility. The BOK could therefore keep 
rates unchanged at this meeting while 
signalling the possibility of a further hike 
at the October meeting. 
Bangko Sentral ng Pilipinas (27 August)

We expect the BSP to raise the 
benchmark rate by 25bp to 5%. Recent 
communication from the Governor has 
been focused on inflation risks, despite 
readings coming off recent highs (but 
remains way higher than target) and 2Q 
growth slowing sharply. Officials would 
likely prefer to see a more convincing 
disinflationary trend before drawing a 
pause. The currency's drift to record 
lows alongside a renewed rise in Brent 
prices adds to the case for a pre-emptive 
tightening response , with the call 
strengthened further by the moderation 
in the foreign reserves position . Beyond 
a likely hike in August, our baseline 
forecast is for one more hike within the 
year before slipping into a prolonged 
pause. Policymakers will monitor signs 
of a potential shift in the US Fed’s policy 
bias as well as US yield movements in the 
near-term.

### Page 3

The Week Ahead August 21, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Exports growth is expected to moderate 
slightly from 53.4% yoy in June to 44.8% 
in July, largely reflecting base effects. 
The moderation is broadly in line with the 
easing in China’s export growth, from 
27.0% yoy in June to 23.9% in July. 
Nevertheless, trade momentum remains 
robust, supported by easing geopolitical 
disruptions following the Middle East 
ceasefire and strong demand for AI -
related electronics. Import growth is also 
expected to remain firm, reflecting  
sustained demand for intermediate and 
capital goods amid resilient export 
orders and a gradual improvement in 
investment sentiment.

Singapore

We expect Singapore’s headline and 
core inflation to rise to 2.4% yoy and 2.3% 
yoy, respectively, in July, from 1.9% yoy 
and 1.6% yoy in June. The pickup was due 
to the delayed pass -through of higher 
global energy costs reflected in the 
sharp 17% increase in electricity tariffs in 
3Q26, alongside firmer food price 
increases. This build -up in underlying 
price pressures likely validated t he 
central bank’s decision to very slightly 
increase the appreciation pace of its 
currency policy band at its July review. 
Policymakers will likely maintain their 
vigilance and a slight hawkish stance 
toward upside inflation risks in their 
commentary on th e inflation outlook, 
given the ongoing disruptions and 
conflict in the Middle East.

We forecast industrial production 
growth to rise to 9.0% yoy in July, from 
7.2% yoy  in June. This was driven 
primarily by robust expansion in the 
electronics and precision engineering 
clusters, both of which continued to be 
underpinned by strong global demand 
for artificial intelligence -related 
hardware. However, factory 
performance remained uneven, as rising 
cost pressures and supply chain 
disruptions in the Strait of Hormuz 
continue to weigh on chemicals and 
general manufacturing clusters.

資料來源：台灣指數公司、國泰證期研究部整理

富世達 6805 TT  ( 電子–電腦及周邊 ) 
液冷升級，富勢續揚 
投資與評價 
1. 富世達 2Q26 受 AI 平台及手機產品轉換影響，營收短期下滑，惟伺服器
快接頭占比提升與製程改善帶動獲利率優於預期 ；3Q26 隨 GB300 維持高
檔、VR200 及 Trainium 3 逐步放量，預期營運創單季新高。 
2. 富世達 26 年受惠 GPU、ASIC 快接頭需求成長，預估營收年增 45.2%、
EPS 62.68 元；27 年隨 Rubin 與 Trainium 3 貢獻完整年度營收，加上
Rubin Ultra 導入 ZQD 規格升級，預估營收年增 60.4%、EPS 110.13 元。 
3. 目前富世達股價交易在 2,155 元(19.6x 27(F) PER )，位於近三年參考本益
比區間 12～38x 中間偏下位置，考量 AI 伺服器液冷需求持續擴張，快接
頭應用範圍逐步擴大，帶動單機櫃 QD 用量、產品價值量與獲利率提升，
維持買進投資建議，維持 25 倍本益比，目標價由 2,260 元(25x 修正前
27(F) PER)調升至 2,750 元(25x 修正後 27(F) PER)。 
重點更新 
平台轉換壓抑出貨動能，富世達 2Q26 營運動能放緩 
富世達 2Q26 受伺服器產品由 GB300 轉換至 VR200、ASIC 平台由 Trainium 
2.5 轉換至 Trainium 3影響 ， 加上手機軸承進入新舊機種交接期 ， 營收為37.40
億元(-10.6% QoQ)，較原先預估低 13.2%。毛利率方面，受惠高毛利 AI 伺服
器快接頭占比提升，以及製程優化與量產效率改善，毛利率季增 1.7 個百分點
至 34.0%，較原先預估高 1.6 個百分點。營業費用為 2.30 億元(-0.8% QoQ)，
亦低於原先預期，帶動營業利益為 10.43 億元(-6.9% QoQ)，營業利益率升至
27.9%；稅後淨利 8.49 億元(-7.4% QoQ)，EPS 12.39 元，反映平台轉換期間
營運動能短期放緩。 
多平台新品逐步放量，預期富世達 3Q26 營運創單季新高   
富世達 3Q26 受惠 GB300 快接頭出貨維持高檔 ，VR200 亦逐步進入量產 ，QD
應用由 Switch Tray 延伸至 Compute Tray 及 Rack Manifold，帶動單機櫃快
接頭用量與價值量提升；ASIC 方面，AWS Trainium 3水冷快接頭開始小量出
貨，滑軌業務亦受惠AWS 新平台進入量產。惟考量VR200 放量時程略晚於預
期，下修3Q26 營收預估 1.8%至 45.85 億元(+22.6% QoQ)。毛利率受惠高毛
利伺服器產品占比提升、 QD 稼動率及製程良率改善，上調 2.7 個百分點至
35.9%。受惠產品組合優化幅度優於原先預期，且營業費用控管得宜，上修營
業利益 8.2%至 13.76 億元(+31.9% QoQ )，稅後淨利 11.27 億元(+32.7% 
QoQ)，EPS 16.44 元，預期營運創單季新高。

報告內容 
平台轉換壓抑出貨動能，富世達 2Q26 營運動能放緩 
富世達 2Q26 伺服器相關產品由 GB300 轉換至 VR200，ASIC 平台亦處於 Trainium 2.5 轉換至
Trainium 3 的交接階段，加上手機軸承進入新舊機種轉換期，使相關產品出貨短期下滑，營收為
37.40 億元(-10.6% QoQ)，較原先預估低 13.2%，顯示平台轉換期對拉貨節奏影響較預期明顯。 
惟本季高毛利 AI 伺服器快接頭營收占比持續提升，加上製程優化及量產效率改善，使產品組合優
化幅度優於預期 ， 毛利率季增1.7 個百分點至 34.05%， 較原先預估高出1.6 個百分點 。 費用方面 ，
營業費用為 2.30 億元(-0.8% QoQ)，與前季大致持平，並較原先預估低7.7%。雖毛利率提升及費
用控管優於預期，部分抵銷營收下滑壓力，惟營業利益仍受營收季減影響降至 10.43 億元(-6.9% 
QoQ)， 稅後淨利為8.49 億元(-7.4% QoQ)，EPS 12.39 元 ， 反映平台轉換期間營運動能短期放緩 。

多平台新品逐步放量，預期富世達 3Q26 獲利創單季新高  
富世達伺服器產品出貨動能可望於 3Q26 明顯回升，GB300 快接頭出貨維持高檔，VR200 相關產
品亦逐步進入量產階段，其中 QD 應用除既有 Switch Tray 外，亦延伸至 Compute Tray 及 Rack 
Manifold，帶動單機櫃快接頭用量與水冷零組件價值量提升。ASIC 業務則受惠 AWS Trainium 3
水冷快接頭自 3Q26 開始小量出貨，後續隨客戶拉貨增加，營收貢獻可望逐月放大；滑軌業務則
受惠通用型伺服器需求維持穩健 ，AWS 新平台開始量產 ，VR 相關專案亦持續進行驗證 。 雖VR200
仍處於平台轉換初期，出貨放量速度較原先預期慢，加上手機軸承拉貨較為保守，使 3Q26 營收
預估下修 1.8%至 45.85 億元，惟仍季增 22.6%，顯示平台轉換後伺服器新品出貨動能逐步恢復。 
隨高毛利伺服器產品營收占比提高、QD 出貨規模擴大，以及製程與量產效率持續改善，產品組合
優於原先假設，故調高毛利率 2.7 個百分點至 35.9%。費用方面，考量營運規模擴大及新平台相
關研發與量產準備支出增加，預估營業費用季增 17.9%至 2.71 億元；惟隨營收規模回升，營業費
用率預期由 2Q26 的 6.1%降至 3Q26 的 5.9%，費用控管仍屬穩健。受惠產品組合優化幅度優於
原先預期，且營業費用控管得宜，故上調營業利益 8.2%至 13.76 億元(+31.9% QoQ)，稅後淨利
季增 32.7%至 11.27 億元，EPS 16.44 元，獲利將創單季新高。

富世達 26 年受惠 AI 液冷需求擴張與新品導入，預期獲利創歷史新高  
26 年 AI 伺服器液冷需求維持高成長趨勢，主要受惠全球CSP 持續擴大 AI 基礎建設資本支出，帶
動 GPU 及 ASIC 平台水冷快接頭需求同步增加。GPU 方面，GB300 Switch Tray快接頭出貨維持
高檔，VR200 相關產品亦逐步進入量產階段；相較前一代平台，VR 除 Switch Tray 及 Compute 
Tray 持續使用 QD 外，應用亦進一步延伸至 Rack Manifold ，帶動單機櫃快接頭用量與水冷零組
件價值量提升。ASIC 方面，Trainium 3 水冷快接頭自 3Q26 開始小量出貨，預期 4Q26 隨客戶拉
貨增加，營收貢獻將進一步放大；Google 等其他 CSP 專案亦持續進行驗證，使公司 QD 成長來
源由單一 GPU 平台逐步擴展至多家 ASIC。通用型伺服器滑軌需求維持穩健，且 AWS 新平台自
3Q26 開始量產，惟因營收占比相對有限，短期對整體成長貢獻仍較有限。考量手機軸承受新舊機
種轉換影響，出貨明顯低於預期，且 VR200 放量時程較原先預期慢，部分抵銷伺服器產品成長動
能，故下修富世達 26 年營收預估 4.4%至 180.23 億元(+45.2% YoY)。  
隨高毛利 AI 伺服器快接頭營收占比持續提高，加上 QD 應用範圍擴大及出貨規模成長，帶動產品
組合進一步優化；同時，既有產品隨量產規模放大，製程成熟度、生產效率及良率持續改善，故調
高 26 年毛利率 1.9 個百分點至 35.0%。產能方面，目前 QD 月產能約 300 萬顆，因訂單能見度
已延伸至 1Q27，新增產能預計於 4Q26 陸續開出，可望支應 VR 及 ASIC 平台後續拉貨需求。費
用方面，考量研發與擴產相關支出低於原先預期，下修 26 年營業費用 4.7%至 10.33 億元。雖營
收預估下修，惟產品組合優化及營業費用控管優於原先預期，帶動獲利預估仍上修，故調高營業利
益 2.2%至 52.67 億元(+108.6% YoY)，稅後淨利為 42.97 億元(+102.2% YoY)，EPS 62.68 元， 
營運創歷史新高。

### Page 4

預期富世達 27 年受惠 Vera Rubin 與 ASIC 貢獻放大，獲利續創歷史新高 
延續 26 年 Vera Rubin 與 AWS Trainium 3 等新平台陸續導入，成長動能將由新專案貢獻完整年
度營收、客戶拉貨規模擴大，以及水冷快接頭規格持續升級所帶動。Vera Rubin 預期於 27 年進
入主要放量階段，隨 AI 伺服器液冷架構由 Tray 逐步向 Rack 層級擴展，單機櫃快接頭用量與水冷
零組件價值量可望進一步提升。下一世代Rubin Ultra 則因晶片功耗及冷卻液流量需求提高，可望
導入更高流量的 ZQD，其中 ZQD 採用球閥設計，以提升冷卻液流量，目前相關產品已進入送樣
與驗證階段。研究部認為升級至 ZQD，不僅反映 AI 伺服器散熱規格持續提高，亦將增加產品設
計、精密加工及可靠度驗證門檻，有助提升富世達單顆快接頭 ASP 與單機櫃價值量，並強化公司
於新世代 AI 液冷供應鏈的競爭優勢。ASIC 方面，AWS Trainium 3 於 26 年下半年開始貢獻後，
27 年出貨規模可望持續擴大，並貢獻完整年度營收；Google 等其他 CSP 客戶的客製化 QD 專案
亦陸續完成驗證與導入，使公司伺服器成長來源由 GPU 平台進一步擴展至多家 ASIC 客戶，降低
單一平台產品轉換對出貨節奏的影響。滑軌業務則延續既有通用型伺服器出貨基礎，並受惠 AWS
相關專案逐步放量，提供另一成長來源。考量 Vera Rubin 與 Trainium 3 全年貢獻、Rubin Ultra
快接頭規格升級 ， 以及其他ASIC 客戶導入進度均優於原先預期 ， 調高富世達27 年營收預估 11.2%
至 289.13 億元(+60.4% YoY)。 
毛利率方面，隨伺服器產品營收占比持續提升，且 QD 具備潛在規格升級動能、高 ASP 快接頭逐
步貢獻，產品組合可望進一步優化；同時 26 年擴增的 QD 產能於 27 年完整投入量產，出貨規模
放大將帶動產能利用率、生產效率及固定成本攤提效益改善，故調高27 年毛利率 1.9 個百分點至
37.0%。隨 Vera Rubin 與 Trainium 3 放量帶動營收規模擴大，加上 ZQD 等高 ASP 產品逐步導
入 、 產能利用率提升及固定成本攤提效益改善 ， 上修營業利益20.4%至 93.57 億元(+77.6% YoY)，
稅後淨利達 75.50 億元(+75.7% YoY)，EPS 110.13 元，營運續創歷史新高。

AI 伺服器快接頭價值提升，富世達中長期成長動能延續  
富世達受惠 AI 伺服器液冷需求持續擴張，隨 GPU 與 ASIC 平台功耗提升、液冷架構由 Tray 層級
延伸至 Rack 層級，快接頭應用範圍由 GB300 Switch Tray 逐步擴大至 VR200 Compute Tray 、
Rack Manifold 及 AWS Trainium 3 等 ASIC 平台，帶動單機櫃 QD 用量與水冷零組件價值量提
升。展望27 年，Vera Rubin進入主要放量階段，Trainium 3 亦將貢獻完整年度營收，Rubin Ultra
則因冷卻液流量需求提高，有望導入更高規格 ZQD，推升單顆快接頭 ASP 與產品技術門檻；加上
Google 等其他 CSP 客製化 QD 專案陸續驗證與導入 ， 伺服器成長來源可望由GPU 進一步擴展至
多家 ASIC 客戶。目前股價交易在 2,155 元，約 19.6x FY27(F) PER ，位於近三年本益比參考區間
12～38x 之中間偏下位置，考量AI 液冷滲透率持續提升、VR 與 ASIC 平台放量能見度提高，以及
ZQD 潛在規格升級將帶動產品組合與獲利率改善，維持25 倍評價，因調升 27 年獲利預估，故目
標價由 2,260 元(25x 修正前 FY27(F) PER)調升至 2,750 元(25x 修正後 FY27(F) PER)。

### Page 5

公司簡介 
富世達股份有限公司（Fositek Corp., 6805 TT）成立於 2001 年，為台灣高精密機構件與軸承設 
計製造廠，總部位於新北市新莊區。公司早期以筆記型電腦、平板電腦等消費性電子轉軸零件起 
家，具備金屬沖壓、精密機構設計、模具開發與量產製造能力，並長期服務國際筆電與手機品牌客 
戶。隨消費性電子產品型態演進，公司逐步由傳統筆電轉軸延伸至摺疊手機軸承，憑藉高精密加 
工、微型化設計與量產良率控管能力，切入左右摺、上下小摺及三摺機等高階摺疊手機應用。 近 
年來，隨 AI 伺服器功耗提升帶動液冷架構快速發展，富世達亦將精密機構件能力延伸至 AI 伺服 
器水冷零組件領域，產品由傳統軸承逐步拓展至快接頭 （QD） 、浮動接頭、伺服器滑軌及相關水冷 
機構件。相較傳統機構件，AI 伺服器快接頭需兼顧低漏液風險、耐壓性、插拔壽命與高可靠度， 
技術與製造門檻較高，亦成為公司未來主要成長動能。 摺疊手機軸承方面，富世達於華為中高階 
摺疊機種維持主要供應地位，其中三摺機因採雙鉸鏈設計，單機軸承價值量高於一般左右摺與上 
下小摺，有助支撐手機軸承營收表現。惟考量整體摺疊手機市場仍屬高階利基型市場，且2026 年 
三摺機已有一定出貨基期，預期手機軸承業務以穩定貢獻為主。伺服器業務方面，受惠 AI 機櫃液 
冷滲透率提升、新平台水冷零組件用量增加，以及ASIC 水冷需求逐步放大，公司快接頭與相關機 
構件營收占比快速提升，並帶動產品組合改善。 富世達目前生產布局以深圳為主要量產與品質檢 
驗據點，並於崑山、合肥等地設有服務據點，可就近支援客戶開發、驗證與量產需求。依應用別分 
類，1H26 營收中，伺服器產品占比約 55.7%，摺疊手機約 38.0%，筆記型電腦約 5.6%，其他約 
0.7%。

### Page 6

富世達 ESG 政策 
富世達以「環境保護、社會責任、公司治理」作為永續經營核心，並將誠信經營、產品品質、供
應 鏈韌性與環境管理納入公司長期發展策略。環境面方面，公司持續推動溫室氣體盤查與能源
管理， 2024 年已將深圳及越南等主要營運據點納入管理範圍，並透過用電管理、節水措施、
廢棄物分類 與污水處理等制度降低營運對環境之影響。雖 2024 年因營收規模擴大及越南廠納
入統計，使總碳 排量增加，惟溫室氣體排放密集度由0.8047 下降至 0.6733 公噸 CO2e／百萬
元，顯示營運效率 與能源使用效率已有改善。隨AI 伺服器水冷快接頭、滑軌及相關機構件逐步
放量 ， 公司亦將持續 強化生產效率 、 自動化製程與節能減碳作為 ， 以兼顧營運成長與環境責任 。

供應鏈管理方面，富世達依循 RBA 責任商業聯盟精神，推動供應商行為準則、有害物質管制、
衝突礦產管理與綠色採購政策。公司要求供應商簽署社會責任承諾書、衝突礦產承諾書及不使
用禁用／限用物質承諾，並遵循 RoHS、REACH、WEEE 等規範，以降低供應鏈環境與人權風
險。2024 年公司進行 73 家供應商稽核，通過率達 100%，並透過包材回收利用及綠色採購推
動供應鏈永續化。未來公司亦規劃提高當地採購比例，短期目標提升至 60%，中長期目標提升
至 80%，以強化供應鏈韌性並降低營運風險。

員工與社會面方面，富世達將職業安全與健康列為 2024 年最重要永續議題，持續落實 ISO 
45001 職業安全衛生管理系統，並透過護理師臨廠服務、職業安全宣導與健康諮詢，維護員工
身心健康。 公司亦重視職場人權與多元平等，推動友善職場、杜絕職場性騷擾、重視兩性平權
與同工同酬， 2024 年共召開 4 場勞資會議，透過多元溝通管道提升員工參與及向心力。社會
公益方面，公司秉 持 「取之於社會，用之於社會」 精神，2024 年捐贈新台幣 5 萬元支持 「1919
愛走動－急難家庭 救助計畫」 ， 協助弱勢及急難家庭度過困境 ， 並規劃持續擴大社會參與範圍 。

公司治理方面，富世達以誠信經營與法規遵循為基礎，持續強化董事會職能、內部控制、風險管
理 及資訊安全。公司已設置審計委員會與薪酬委員會，並於 2024 年安排董事法律遵循與公司
治理相 關進修課程，合計進修時數達51 小時。2024 年公司未發生重大違反法規事件或支付違
規罰款， 並持續透過教育訓練與內部宣導推動商業道德、公司治理與員工品德操守。資訊安全
方面，公司已 導入 ISO 27001 資訊安全管理制度，並規劃台北、深圳及越南各據點逐步取得
相關認證 ， 以提升 客戶資料 、 研發資訊與營運系統之防護能力 。 整體而言 ， 富世達以治理透明 、
供應鏈管理、職安人 權與環境效率改善作為永續發展主軸，並配合 AI 伺服器與高階機構件業
務成長，逐步建立更具韌 性的長期營運基礎。

### Page 1

2026 年 9 月 8 日 • 產業概況更新

重電產業

AI 伺服器單櫃功率持續提升，看好模組化 DC 需求強勁成長

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。

陳澤心 
Amber.Chen53@yuanta.com 
陳亮齊 
chrischenlc@yuanta.com

元大觀點 
⧫  
AI 與 半導 體擴 產、 強韌 電網需 求帶 動，2025 年 實質民 間資 本成 長
26%，輸發配電設備年增 12%，2026 年動能持續。 
⧫  
為加速 AI 資料中心交期，模組化預製電站加速部署，2035 年市場規模
估達 1,280 億美元，2025-2035 年 CAGR 達 14%。 
⧫  
宏于電機與施耐德、士電深度合作 IEC/UL 預製電站進攻 AI 資料中心商
機，宏于將與士電、華城等同業一同受惠長期電力升級趨勢。 
AI/半導體需求帶動輸發配電設備穩健成長 
根據主計處與經濟部資料顯示，2025 年實質民間資本年增 26% 至 2.2 兆台
幣，主要受惠於 AI 與半導體擴產、資料中心建置及電力基礎設施投資。隨
政府大力推動綠能發展及強韌電網計畫，加上 AI 高效能運算蓬勃發展，帶動
輸發配電設備強勁需求，2025 年實質民間資本成長 26%，輸發配電設備年
增 12%，2026 年動能持續，帶動重電與配電設備產業進入高速成長期。 
AI 伺服器單櫃功率持續提升，模組化 DC 有效解決安全瓶頸 
生成式 AI 帶動全球資料中心耗電量快速攀升，國際能源署 IEA 預估 2030 年
全球資料中心耗電將突破 1,000 TWh，CAGR 達 15%；全台資料中心電力容
量亦預期由 2025 年的 303 MW 倍增至 2030 年的 900 MW。由於 AI 高算力
使單機櫃功率密度飆升至 100 kW 乃至 1 MW ，傳統磚造建置期長且面臨散
熱與電弧安全瓶頸，推升預製模組化資料中心 (Prefabricated MDC )市場由 
2025 年的 293 億美元倍增至 2035 年的 1,280 億美元，CAGR 達 13.8%，
能縮減 6 個月以上工期的模組化架構將成為未來趨勢。 
重電族群同迎電網升級浪潮，宏于結盟大廠切入 AI 資料中心 
台灣輸配電與高低壓配電設備市場 迎接強韌電網與規格升級商機，帶動 宏于
電機、士電、華城、中興電、東元 、亞力 等廠商 共同受惠。 其中，宏于
1H26 營收 17.7 億元，毛利率 23.2%，與 1H25 營收 17.1 億元，毛利率
18.2%相比，產品組合與獲利能力轉好。此外宏于持續布局新產品如 1) 預製
型貨櫃變電站搶攻 AI 商機，預計於 2027 年底啟動試產出貨；2) 與施耐德合
作 Okken 配電盤搶攻雲端大廠與半導體供應鏈需求並已具銷售實績； 3) 布
局 UL 認證預計 2027 取證強攻美國算力基建；4) 越南新廠已於 3Q26 投產逐
步擴大東南亞市場；5) 微電網示範案場已完成逐步帶動營收規模提升。在多
元布局帶動下，看好宏于持續受惠上述商機，長期營收獲利有望持續創高。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：重電 2026 年 9 月 8 日  第2頁，共10頁   
產業分析  
1-7M26 輸發配電設備年增 2%，AI/半導體需求強 
根據主計處資料顯示，2025 年實質民間資本(民間部門在建築、機械設備、運輸工具等固定資產新
增投資) 年增 26%至 2.2 兆台幣，主要受惠 AI 與半導體擴產、資料中心與電力基礎設施投資、汰舊
換新等。此外 2025 年隨政府大力推動綠色能源發展及強韌電網計畫，加上 AI 、高效能運算等新興
科技應用蓬勃發展，資料中心建置快速增長，以及美國電網擴建工程持續推進，帶動相關輸發配電
設備之需求，2026 年 1-7 月產業產值年增 2% 至 758.6 億元，其中高低壓配電產值約占 19%，有
利宏于電機、亞力、華城、士電、東元、中興電等重電廠商。 
圖 1：2025 年民間固定資本成長 26% 圖 2：1-7M26 輸/發電及配電機械業產值年增 2%

資料來源：主計處、元大投顧 資料來源：經濟部、元大投顧

強韌電網計畫帶動電力規格由 CNS 舊制轉向國際 IEC 規格 
台電於 2022 年 9 月正式公布「強化電網韌性建設計畫」，規劃 10 年內投入 5,645 億元，打造分
散式電網，並搭配再生能源儲能發展，達到  2050 淨零轉型目標，目標希望台灣未來不再因事故而
發生大範圍、長時間的停電，此計畫對於變電站和輸配電線路、線纜更新及地下化的相關需求龐大。
台電也持推動電力規格由 CNS 舊制轉為國際 IEC 規格，對於電力設備之精度、容忍值、安全性、
資訊監控等規格持續提高，所有標案無論新舊設備均持續汰換並符合國際 IEC 規格。宏于電機為業
界首家導入 IEC 規格之配電盤廠，具專利保護，為強韌電網趨勢下受 惠廠商。 
圖 3：台電強韌電網計畫可分為分散、強固、防衛三大工程

資料來源：主計處、元大投顧

### Page 3

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：重電 2026 年 9 月 8 日  第3頁，共10頁   
AI 浪潮推升資料中心用電需求，表後儲能市場呈爆發式成長 
國際能源署 IEA 指出，受惠於數位化與生成式  AI 的爆發性成長，過去五年全球資料中心用電量
CAGR 達 13% ，而 2025 年全球資料中心耗電量約 486TWh ，占全球用電量 1.7%，未來 AI 模型
訓練與推論的運算耗能遠高於傳統應用，資料中心的冷卻系統亦會加劇能源消耗，皆會進一步推升
電力需求。預估至 2030 年，全球資料中心耗電將超過 1,000TWh，占比升至 3%，CAGR 達 15%。
台灣亦呈現相同趨勢，截至 2025 年全台資料中心耗電量約 303 MW ，MIC 預期 2030 年台灣資料
中心電力容量將大幅成長 197% 至 900 MW。 
表後儲能是指在電力公司的電表後方 (用戶端)設置儲能設備，企業可於離峰時段充電、尖峰時段放
電，實現節省電費及削峰填谷的目的。根據 Straits Research 資料，由於 1) 分散式能源裝置快速普
及；2) 能源價格和韌性需求提升；3) 智慧電錶、IoT 和 AI 能源管理系統的技術進步等因素，全球
表後能源(Behind-the-Meter，BTM)市場成長動能強勁。全球表後市場規模於 2025 年為 76 億美元，
預計於 2034 年擴張至 395.7 億美元，2026-2034 年 CAGR 達 20.12%。 
圖 4：全球資料中心耗電量 CAGR 達 15% 圖 5：全球表後能源市場 CAGR 達 20%

資料來源：IEA、元大投顧 資料來源：Straits Research、元大投顧 
模組化資料中心加速 AI 資料中心部署， 2035 年市場規模估將達 1,280 億美元 
全球預製模組化資料中心(Prefabricated Modular Data Centers ，MDC)市場規模預計將由 2025 年
的 293 億美元倍增至 2035 年的 1,280 億美元，CAGR 達 13.8%，主要受惠 AI 驅動算力暴增，單
機櫃功率密度由傳統 5～10 kW 急遽飆升至 100 kW 乃至 1 MW，導致可用故障電流激增，提高電
弧閃絡風險、散熱熱負荷過載，以及液冷系統易燃液體洩漏等重大火災隱患；面對傳統磚造施工週
期冗長與各項安全挑戰，預製模組化架構將電力、冷卻與  IT 系統於工廠預製整合並通過結構與安
全測試，不僅能大幅加速部署進度、簡化地方主管機關審批流程，更可支援 MW 規模的彈性擴充，
成為解決高功耗安全瓶頸與縮短建置時程的解方。 
圖 6：模組化資料中心市場規模穩健成長，CAGR 達 14%

資料來源：UL、元大投顧

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：重電 2026 年 9 月 8 日  第4頁，共10頁   
宏于電機公司簡介 
宏于電機為國內智能配電盤領導廠商 
宏于電機成立於 1987年，為台灣智能配電盤領導廠商，於重電產業擁有近 40年經驗，提供配電系
統設計、製造、安裝到維護的一條龍服務。產品符合  IEC 安全規範與 ISO 品質標準，涵蓋高、低
壓配電盤，並結合自主開發的 iPanel 電力雲監控。系統可 24 小時監控設備狀態，提升安全性、降
低故障與維護成本達50%以上，協助企業打造高效、安全的電力管理環境。 
此外，宏于同時為全台最大銅排進口商及專業 ODM 設計加工廠，市占已達全台 50%以上，目前
1,700 噸年產能為第二名的三倍。公司客戶遍及半導體、電信、醫療、太陽能、電力、建設、公共
建設等產業，於台灣智能配電盤市場深耕已久，其產品為國內各大政府機構與科技業工廠等所採用。  
公司三大事業為智能電力(智能配電盤)、電力導體(銅排 ODM) 以及能源系統(電力工程)，近年致力
於提升傳統產品附加價值，例如開發智能和低碳配電盤、AI 雲端中心使用之貨櫃型變電站，微電網
工程進攻表後儲能市場，並與國際電力巨頭施耐德於台灣授權生產旗艦產品 OKKEN 低壓配變盤等
等。針對北美與國際市場，公司推出貨櫃型預製式變 電站，可大幅縮短資料中心建置時程，可望受
惠全球資料中心電力基礎建設浪潮，且越南新廠已於 3Q26 開始接單生產貢獻營收，共同挹注中長
期營收動能。主要客戶涵蓋半導體與科技廠，東元、士電等國內機電大廠，以及台電、中鋼、桃園
機場等重大公務機關；產品別營收比重約為智能電力  40-45%、電力導體 40-45% 及能源系統 10-
15%。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：重電 2026 年 9 月 8 日  第5頁，共10頁   
圖 8：2025 年產品別分布

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 
配電盤以高規格及策略聯盟創造差異化，領先業界邁入國際 IEC 規格 
宏于電機於國內配電盤市場處於領先的地位， 不僅為全台首家完全符合新式 CNS 15156 標準的配
電盤大廠，更通過國家級耐震 7 級嚴苛測試，具備領先同業的換規優勢。此外，為解決國內電機技
師多不了解  IEC 新制的痛點，宏于以自行研發的配電設計系統 (iPanelCAD)，協助技師快速適應
IEC 新制，並在系統中導入策略聯盟的產品與 BOM 表。宏于每年投入研發與認證金額佔營收 5-8%
且逐年提升，目前法規對配電相關產品新形式均需送審， 每件費用高達 800-1,000 萬台幣，費用高
昂，對規模小之廠商產生進入障礙。相較台灣 200 多家價格落差大、服務保固與品質不一的配電盤
業者，宏于憑藉技術與認證優勢，吸引國內外大廠(施耐德、三菱、士電)建立策略聯盟。 
宏于智能配電盤採業界最高標準設計，具專利保護且已規劃完成 IEC 61439、IEC 62271 及 UL 891 
等國際標準認證佈局。台灣大大小小的配電盤業者多達 200 多家，各家廠牌價格落差且服務保固不
同，甚至品質不穩定等等情況。基於以上優勢，國內外大廠(施耐德/三菱/士電)與宏于建立策略聯盟，
以高端品牌櫃成功創造出與同業差異。 
而在智能化軟體方面，宏于將  AI 技術導入配電盤，推出「iPanel 雲端管理系統」，突破傳統被動
保護模式，賦予設備大數據分析與即時監控能力，可穩定供電並進行線上控制。透過  AI 演算精準
診斷電力狀況並自動優化供電效率，實現斷電預測與預防，iPanel 大幅提升系統可靠度，降低 50% 
維運人力成本，並強化客戶黏著度。 
圖 9：宏于智能配電盤具高耐銹性、防爆安全及模組化設計等優勢

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧

圖 10：配電盤產品成本組成
